  - The trusted hops are the valid entries of the `whitelist` setting (`getTrustedProxies()` in `src/express-common.js`). `whitelist.txt` and the Docker host addresses do not count. `getForwardedIps()` returns the address in each enabled forwarded header and reads `X-Forwarded-For` from the right: it skips trusted hops, and the first other entry is the client.
  - With `enableForwardedWhitelist` on, the whitelist blocks a request unless every address that `getForwardedIps()` returns is listed, and it names the first unlisted one.
  - `getIpAddress()` adds a forwarded address only when the socket address is a trusted hop and every enabled header names the same valid address. Otherwise it returns the socket address. The basic auth, account login, account recovery, password reset and conversation message limiters and the access log read their address through it.
  - `isIpInPatterns()` parses addresses strictly and treats a value that is not an IP address as unlisted, so a malformed forwarded value or a socket without an address gets 403, not 500. An IPv4-mapped value matches its IPv4 entry.
  - `requireHttps` lets plain HTTP through only from a loopback socket address without forwarding headers, whatever the `Host` header says. It reads the first `X-Forwarded-Proto` value, and only from a trusted hop.
  - Basic auth matches no request when the configured username or password is empty or is not a string, and it compares the configured user with `crypto.timingSafeEqual()` over SHA-256 digests.
  - `hostWhitelist.enabled` goes through the `'boolean'` converter, so `SILLYTAVERN_HOSTWHITELIST_ENABLED=false` turns the host check off.
- **Tests:** `tests/forwarded-client-address.test.js`, `tests/require-https-client-address.test.js`, `tests/basic-auth-configured-credentials.test.js`, `tests/host-whitelist-enabled-switch.test.js`, `tests/middleware-whitelist.test.js`, `tests/middleware-host-whitelist.test.js`, `tests/middleware-basic-auth.test.js`, `tests/middleware-require-https.test.js`.
- **Fixes:** `1b127a865` (whitelist), `06dc7a6e0` (rate-limit keys), `e059e5d23` (`requireHttps`), `0a836ed40` (basic auth credentials), `30ecce06d` (host whitelist switch).

### Temporary response lengths

- **Breaks:** the Response (tokens) preset, or the Chat Completion max tokens, keeps the response length of a background request. The next settings save stores that length, and later requests use it as the preset.
- **Cause:** `generateRaw()`, `generateRawData()` and `generateQuietPrompt()` in `public/script.js` put a `responseLength` in `amount_gen`, or in `oai_settings.openai_max_tokens` for Chat Completion, until the request data is built. `TempResponseLength` kept one saved value. The class comes from SillyTavern (`749c5df29`), and SillyTavern's `staging` still had the single saved value at `ad29cbda6`. A second call that started in that time saved the temporary length of the first call as the original. The first restore wrote that length back and the second restore did nothing, so `isCustomized()` answered false and `saveSettingsInner()` did not wait. When a Chat Completion call overlapped a call to another API, a setting could also get the saved value of the other setting. Calls can overlap when they run at the same time, as the replies of a Conversation Mode group DM do (`Promise.allSettled()` in `public/scripts/sillybunny-conversation/attachments.js`, through `generateRaw()` with `reply_max_tokens`), and they overlap when an event listener of one call starts another call.
- **Files:** `public/script.js` (upstream), `public/scripts/generation-response-length-overrides.js`.
- **Guards:** `TempResponseLength.save()` returns the override of its call. A call removes its own override after it builds its request data or in its `finally` block. `createResponseLengthOverrides()` keeps the overrides in the order of their calls. A setting holds the length of its newest override and gets its own value back when its last override is removed, in any order of removal, and the two settings have separate saved values. `restore(null)`, the forced restore in `saveSettingsInner()`, removes every override.
- **Guards:** `CHAT_COMPLETION_SETTINGS_READY` and `GENERATE_AFTER_DATA` do not name the generation that emitted them. A quiet prompt waits for its event from its start, and a raw Chat Completion call from just before it builds its request data. One event removes one override: the one that started to wait last. A call that runs inside another call starts to wait last and reaches its event first. If an event removed every waiting override, an outer quiet prompt would send the preset after the request of an inner call. If it removed the override that was saved last, the event of a raw call would remove the override of a quiet prompt that started after the raw call saved its length.
- **Tests:** `tests/generation-response-length-overrides.test.js`, `tests/story-mode-request-controls.test.js` (`overlapping temporary response lengths`, each case on Chat Completion, Text Completion, KoboldAI, AI Horde and NovelAI: raw calls that nest, interleave or start together; a quiet prompt inside a raw call or a quiet prompt, around a raw call, and after a raw call that still waits; calls without a length; Stop; the forced restore). 51 of the 56 tests in that block fail with the `script.js` of `4e82939a7`.
- **Limit:** a request still reads its length from the setting. While calls overlap and neither runs inside the other, a request can go out with the length of another call: with two raw calls for 100 and 200 tokens that interleave or start together, both requests carried 200. Only the preset is protected in every order.
- **Limit:** an event from a generation that holds no override also removes one waiting override, so a quiet prompt can send the preset after the Chat Completion request of a call without a length, as it did with the single saved value. With two waiting overrides the later events then remove the override of another call. With a quiet prompt for 100 tokens, a quiet prompt for 200 tokens inside it and a raw Chat Completion call without a length inside that one, the raw call sent 200, the inner quiet prompt 100 and the outer quiet prompt the preset. With the `script.js` of `4e82939a7` the outer quiet prompt sent 100, its own length, and the preset stayed at 100.
- **Fixes:** `98b876ff8` (overlapping temporary response lengths), `5e54d5056` (an event removes the override that started to wait last).

### Slash commands and STscript

- **Breaks:** the STscript parser reads a script differently from how it is written. A closing mark, a pipe or an escaped character is skipped or taken twice, so a command ends up in another command's value, a value gains text such as `undefined` or `[Closure]`, or the parser throws a `TypeError` instead of a parser error.
- **Cause:** the parser moves one index through the text. Branches that return from a nested parse (a nested block comment, the quoted rest of a split-count command, an unclosed quote in lenient parsing) took one more character. Positions were counted back from an index that had already moved past whitespace or a closing quote. The split branches lost an escaped first character, because the parser took it before it started to split.
- **Files:** `public/scripts/slash-commands/SlashCommandParser.js`, `public/scripts/slash-commands/SlashCommandParserError.js`.
- **Guards:** every `SlashCommandParserError` carries the text and the index. The error handler of `executeSlashCommandsWithOptions()` reads its line, column and hint, and without them it throws a `TypeError` instead of showing the parser error.
- **Tests:** `tests/frontend/StscriptParserFixes.e2e.js` (one test or more for each fix, through `tests/frontend/stscript-parser-harness.js`).
- **Limit:** macro positions are still counted back from the end of a value, so a macro is indexed one character late for each escape that follows it in the value. With `REPLACE_GETVAR`, the macro index describes the replaced text, not the source.
- **Open:** `SlashCommandExecutionError.hint` slices its source as the parser error did, so an execution error on a line that another line follows shows the line without its last character (`/let x 1 | /let x 2` followed by a new line shows `/let x `).
- **Fixes:** `15b28ab1b` (nested block comments), `cebf47278` (`/let` with an empty `key=`), `627eb8cc8` (split argument error without text), `aa6f61ce1` (escaped first split value), `b420e832d` (`/parser-flag` without a name), `996ce24cf` (quote flags for closures), `f3e74ca1d` (quoted split-count rest), `87bd300e0` (closure in a joined rest), `cfc4fcf73` (macro positions in quoted values), `5e17bdc22` (unclosed quote in lenient parsing), `039e44a8e` (unknown command position), `643ad824a` (error hint line).

### Conversation Mode response length

- **Breaks:** AI Horde refuses the Conversation Mode requests for a reply, a memory summary and a schedule, so a direct message gets no reply (issue #979).
- **Cause:** Conversation Mode sends its own response length with each request: `reply_max_tokens` for a reply (default `DEFAULT_CONVERSATION_REPLY_MAX_TOKENS`, 16000), `MEMORY_SUMMARY_RESPONSE_TOKENS` (32000) for a memory summary and `SCHEDULE_GENERATION_RESPONSE_TOKENS` (8000) for a schedule. `generateRawData()` in `public/script.js` sends that length as `max_length`, without the worker adjustment that `Generate()` makes for a normal chat. AI Horde refuses a request at submission with "No online worker can serve this request" when `max_length` is above the limit of every worker that serves the model (`horde/exceptions.py` in Haidra-Org/AI-Horde).
- **Files:** `public/scripts/sillybunny-conversation/response-length.js`, `public/scripts/sillybunny-conversation/generation.js`, `public/scripts/sillybunny-conversation/constants.js`.
- **Guards:** every Conversation Mode request from the browser goes through `generateConversationRaw()`, which asks `getActiveApiResponseLength()` for the length before it sends a request to the active API. When that API is AI Horde, the function lowers the length to the preset's Response (tokens), and to the worker limit from `adjustHordeGenerationParams()` when `horde_settings.auto_adjust_response_length` is on, which is the length that a normal chat sends. A request that asks for less keeps its length, and every other API keeps the length. A request through a scoped connection profile also keeps its own length, because `ConnectionManagerRequestService` accepts only Chat Completion and Text Completion profiles. The Conversation REST API does not reach AI Horde: `runBackendGeneration()` in `src/endpoints/conversation-generation.js` calls only the Chat Completion and Text Completion backends.
- **Tests:** `tests/sillybunny-conversation-response-length.test.js` (the lengths for AI Horde and for the other APIs), `tests/sillybunny-conversation-core-generation-regressions.test.js` (the request uses the length), `tests/conversation-mode-scoped-profile.test.js` (the scoped request keeps its length), `tests/story-mode-request-controls.test.js` (a raw AI Horde request sends its length as `max_length`).
- **Limit:** only Conversation Mode has the guard. AI Horde still refuses another `generateRaw()` caller that asks for more than the workers give. `generateRawData()` also sends `max_context` as it is, so `horde_settings.auto_adjust_context_length` does not change a raw request.
- **Limit:** the guard reads the preset from `amount_gen`, as `Generate()` does. `TempResponseLength.save()` in `public/script.js` puts the length of another `generateRaw()` or `generateQuietPrompt()` call in `amount_gen` while that call runs, so a request that starts in that time takes that length for the preset.
- **Fixes:** `fadb0e48b` (issue #979).

### World Info scanning

- **Breaks:** a lorebook entry activates when its keys and settings say it should not, stays off when they say it should activate, or activates and never reaches the prompt.
- **Files:** `public/scripts/world-info.js` (upstream), `public/scripts/world-info-scan-core.js`, `public/scripts/world-info-scan-chat.js`, `public/scripts/world-info-character-book.js`.
- **Tests:** `tests/world-info-scan-core.test.js`, `tests/world-info-scan-chat.test.js`, `tests/world-info-scan-target.test.js`, `tests/world-info-character-book.test.js`, `tests/world-info-activation.test.js` (the activation rules in `tests/util/world-info-cases.js`), `tests/world-info-harness.test.js` (the harness stops with the name of a declaration that `world-info.js` renames or removes, and its constants match the file), `tests/frontend/WorldInfoActivation.e2e.js`.
- **Limit:** the Jest suites run `checkWorldInfo()` and its helpers cut out of `world-info.js` by `tests/util/world-info-harness.js`, with one token per word and settings that each test sets. `tests/frontend/WorldInfoActivation.e2e.js` runs seven rows of the table through the real module, tokenizer and macro engine.
- **Fixes:** #393 (legacy string positions activated but never reached the prompt), `c11467f9b` and `612ea61d6` (scanning and serialization of lorebook entries).

### Markdown auto-fix

- **Breaks:** with Auto-fix Markdown on, nested emphasis loses the spaces around it, a `*` list marker joins the bold label of its item, `* * *` turns into `** **`, and code in a fence or in backticks loses spaces or gains a `*` (issue #955).
- **Cause:** upstream `fixMarkdown()` in `public/scripts/power-user.js` matched pairs with the lazy regex `([*_]{1,2})([\s\S]*?)\1` across the whole message and deleted the horizontal whitespace next to every `*` and `_` inside each match. A match could end inside a `**` run, cross lines and reach into code. The display pass then added `*` or `"` to each line with an odd count, bullet lines and code lines included. `cleanUpMessage()` in `public/script.js` runs the first pass on the reply text that is saved, so stored messages changed too.
- **Files:** `public/scripts/power-user.js` (upstream), `public/scripts/auto-fix-markdown.js`.
- **Guards:** `fixMarkdown()` returns `autoFixMarkdown()`. It works line by line, pairs a whole run of `*` or `_` only with the nearest opener of the same character and length, and deletes only the horizontal whitespace directly inside a pair. A run that follows whitespace closes the open pair of its kind when punctuation or a quote follows it, as in `*She smiles *"Hello."`, which upstream also repairs; before a letter or a digit it opens a nested pair, as in `*a *b* c*`. Unpaired runs, such as list markers, stay. It skips fenced code (a fence that does not close runs to the end of the text, as in a reply that is still streaming), inline code, escaped marks, `_` between letters or digits, and lines made only of `*`, `_`, `-` and whitespace. The display pass skips the same code and lines, and does not count a `*` bullet marker at the start of a line.
- **Tests:** `tests/auto-fix-markdown.test.js` (the issue cases, a comparison with the upstream function on ordinary text, a check that stored text only loses horizontal whitespace, and a time budget for long lines), `tests/auto-fix-markdown-wiring.test.js` (runs `fixMarkdown()` from the source of `power-user.js`).
- **Limit:** pairs do not cross lines, so `*one\ntwo *` keeps its space. A list item whose text has a lone `*` with whitespace on both sides pairs it with the marker, as upstream does: `* 5 * 3 = 15` becomes `*5* 3 = 15`.
- **Limit:** a fence that follows `> ` or a list marker on its line, and an indented code block, are not read as code, so `*` with spaces inside them is paired as in prose. Upstream does the same.
- **Limit:** a run of `*` or `_` with whitespace on both sides pairs with the next run of the same length, so `Name: ___ Age: ___` loses the spaces between the runs. Upstream does the same for every length except four and eight.
- **Limit:** the display pass counts an escaped `\*` as a mark, and a line with an odd count of both `*` and `"` gets only the `"`. Both match upstream.
- **Fixes:** `617492876` (pairing module), `029a740e6` (`fixMarkdown()` adapter).

### Row menus and reordering

- **Breaks:** lists built their own Move items: some showed them disabled at the ends, the group member menu kept them under a tag filter, where a move changes an order that is partly hidden, and only the prompt editor announced the new position.
- **Files:** `public/scripts/sillybunny-editor/reorder-menu.js` and its adopters: `sillybunny-prompt-editor.js`, `extensions/in-chat-agents/ui/agent-list.js` (with `agentMovePosition()` in `agent-list-model.js`), `sillybunny-world-info-editor.js`, `group-chats.js` (`onGroupMemberMenuClick`), `sillybunny-tag-manager.js`, `sillybunny-folder-manager.js`.
- **Guards:**
  - `reorderMenuItems()` builds the move section; an item that cannot act at the row's position is left out, and the whole section while a search, a filter or a non-manual sort is active (the adopter passes `filtered`). The adopter keeps its own data change and save in `move(to)`; a `move` that returns `false` is not announced.
  - `announceReorder()` writes to a polite status region inside the topmost modal dialog, because a modal dialog makes the body inert and its announcements are skipped; keyboard and drag moves use it too.
- **Tests:** `tests/unit/sillybunny-reorder-menu.test.js`; the adopters' unit tests and `tests/prompt-manager-characterization.e2e.js`, `tests/in-chat-agents-list.e2e.js`, `tests/group-editor-member-lists.e2e.js`, `tests/tag-manager.e2e.js`.
- **Upstream sortables:** `attachSortableKeys()` and `adoptUpstreamSortables()` in `reorder-menu.js` give the jQuery UI sortables of upstream code (regex, Quick Reply, logit bias, the sampler orders) Alt+Arrow, Alt+Home and Alt+End moves. `adoptUpstreamSortables()` wraps the sortable widget's `_create`, so it must run before the first list is created (`firstLoadInit` in `public/script.js`, after `batchSortableHandleClasses()`), and a list that destroys and creates its sortable on every display (logit bias) is attached again each time. The helper reads `items`, `handle`, `cancel`, `disabled`, `update` and `stop` at the key press and runs the list's own `stop` with `ui.item`, so the list saves as a drag would; the logit bias lists invert their saved order in that handler, so no case of their own is needed. It shares the module with the row menu helper because both load at startup and each module is a startup request (`tests/frontend-performance.e2e.js`).
- **One tab stop per sortable:** `attachSortableKeys()` gives the first focus target of a list `tabindex="0"` and every other target `-1`; the focused target becomes the stop (`focusin`), and Arrow Up and Down (no modifier) and Home and End move the focus inside the list, so a regex list with hundreds of scripts is one Tab stop. A tabindex that a row already had is never changed, and a plain arrow key from inside a row control is left alone because only a decorated target answers it. Tests: `tests/unit/sillybunny-sortable-keys.test.js` ("one tab stop per list"), `tests/sortable-keys.e2e.js`.
- **Alt+Home and Alt+End on every list that has Alt+Arrow (F-371), keys for the Tag Manager and Folders (F-369), focus after a World Info move (F-370):** `bindListKeyboard()` (`list-rows.js`) passes `onMove` a direction of `-1`, `1`, `-Infinity` (Alt+Home) or `Infinity` (Alt+End), so each adopter must insert the row at the clamped place, not swap two neighbours: `moveEntryBy()` (World Info), `move()` (Tag Manager) and `moveAgentWithinSubheading()` now splice. The ends mean the whole list, past the loaded batches and pages (`revealId` in the Tag Manager, `entryRowAfterMove()` in World Info goes to the row's page). A move that does not change the place returns nothing and is not announced. The list builds its rows again after a save, so the adopter finds the moved row by its key and focuses it (`sillybunny-world-info-editor.js` `moveEntryByKey()`; the Tag Manager and Folders do it in `onMove`). The Tag Manager's `.tag_view_item` also matches the hidden upstream template, so select real rows with `[data-sb-list-key]`. Move to Top and Move to Bottom appear in the row menus from `LONG_LIST` rows. Tests: `tests/audit/list-reorder-keys.e2e.js`.
- **The tracker panel's grip opens the move menu:** the companion panel's sections (`companion-panel.js`, SillyBunny markup) have no row menu, so a click on a section's `.ica--tpanel-drag-handle` opens `openActionMenu()` with `reorderMenuItems()` (Move Up and Move Down, left out at the ends), and `applyPanelReorder()` saves the new order through the same path as a drag. Its unit test mocks `sillybunny-action-menu.js`, because that module reads `translate` from `i18n.js`. Test: `tests/in-chat-agents-companion-panel.e2e.js` ("the grip of a section opens a menu").
- **Open:** the labels follow the Capitalisation Rule (`Move Up`, `Move Down`), and the locale files hold only `Move up` and `Move down`, so group, tag and World Info menus show the English labels in other languages (finding F-269).
- **Long entity lists are one Tab stop (F-360):** each character row took three Tab stops (the row, Edit, Actions), about 90 presses for 30 characters, and the arrow keys moved only inside the lists that used `bindListKeyboard`. `bindListFocus()` (`public/scripts/sillybunny-editor/list-focus.js`) follows libadwaita's `AdwSidebar` (`GTK_LIST_TAB_ITEM`): the current row has `tabindex="0"`, every other row and every control inside it `-1` (the original `tabindex` of a control is kept in a `WeakMap` and comes back when its row becomes current), so Tab enters on the current row, passes through that row's own controls and leaves the list. Up and Down, Home, End, Page Up and Page Down move between rows; in a flow layout (a grid of cards, found by two rows sharing a top) Left and Right move between cards and Up and Down by a line. Alt with an arrow is left to the caller's move. `bindListKeyboard()` binds it for its rows (`singleTabStop: false` keeps the old Tab order for a short list, as the connections of a persona), and the lists that do not use it call it themselves: Chat History, the Data Bank, the swipe picker, the Chats Archive and the member lists of the Tag Manager and the Folders window. The current row is the one focus was last in, else the selected one (`aria-current` or `is-selected`), else the first that is shown; a row that is hidden, `inert` or `aria-disabled` is skipped and loses the stop. A control row without a stop of its own (a member row whose only control is Remove) is entered at its first control. Show More is a `trailingStops` entry: Down from the last row reaches it, and a press from the keyboard (a click with `detail` 0) moves focus to the first row that the press added; a mouse press leaves focus where it is. Shift+Tab into a list that has no visited or selected row enters on the last row. A focused row is scrolled into view before it takes focus, because the character list draws a row it has not shown (`content-visibility: auto`) only when it is on screen. The visibility test falls back to the `hidden` and `inert` attributes in a document with no layout (jsdom), where `getClientRects()` is always empty. Tests: `tests/list-keyboard.e2e.js` (Characters in list and grid, Chromium and WebKit, 1440 and 390), `tests/audit/list-keyboard.e2e.js` (every other family, under the audit config; the Alt+Arrow move of the prompt list), `tests/unit/sillybunny-list-focus.test.js`, `tests/unit/sillybunny-list-rows.test.js`, `tests/sillybunny-chats-archive.e2e.js` ("the sidebar and the list follow the arrow, Home, End and Enter keys": Down reaches Show More and Enter loads the next page, where Down used to load it).
- **A grip is a pointer handle; a keyboard interactable row is its own Tab stop (F-367, F-368):** the prompt list's and World Info's grips were `role="button"` with no `tabindex`, so assistive technology announced a control that Tab never reached while the row already carried Alt+Up and Alt+Down; they are now plain handles (`title` for the pointer, no role or label), as the Tag Manager's and the Folders window's already were (those two have no keyboard move yet). The agents list keeps its focusable grip: it has its own arrow handler and the agent menu offers Move Up and Move Down. In the swipe picker `keyboard.js` gave `.swipe_picker_block` a `tabindex` of its own, so the row and its full-width select button were two stops with one ring; the row now has `not_focusable`, which leaves it without a `tabindex`, so `bindListFocus()` enters the list at the select button. Guards: `tests/audit/list-keyboard-followups.e2e.js`.

### Search entries

- **Breaks:** a bare input inside a wrapper that draws the field takes the focus border and ring that every text input gets from upstream and from the shell roots in `sillybunny-theme.css` (`:is(.sb-shell-root, …) … :focus:not([disabled]):not([readonly])`, specificity 0,5,1), so a second ring appears inside the wrapper's.
- **Files:** `public/scripts/sillybunny-editor/search-entry.js`; the `.sb-search-entry` rules in `public/css/sillybunny-tabs.css`.
- **Guards:**
  - The wrapper draws the field and its focus (`:focus-within`); the inner input's rule (`.sb-search-entry > input.sb-search-entry__input[type='search']`) outranks the shell focus rules and sets no border or shadow.
  - The entry has a height, not a minimum, because `createListSearch` animates the height when it reveals a list's search bar; `list-search.js` measures the field when the height is a token.
  - The clear button shows through `:has(> input:placeholder-shown)`, so the component gives an input without a placeholder a single space as one; a value cleared by a script hides the button too.
- **Tests:** `tests/unit/sillybunny-search-entry.test.js`; `tests/foundation/shared-ui.e2e.js` (search entries).

### Extension settings: adapters for upstream rows and radios

- **Regex script rows are shared entity rows:** `adoptRegexScriptRow()` and `adoptRegexScriptList()` in `public/scripts/sillybunny-editor/adapters/utility-dialogs.js` run in `loadRegexScripts()` after the template's handlers are bound. They move the template's nodes (grip, name, selection checkbox) into a `.ds-entity-row`, add one switch (`input.sb-switch.regex-script-switch`, on when the script is enabled) and one menu button, and keep every upstream control, hidden, in the row: the switch writes `.disable_regex` and sends its `input` event, and the menu items click the template's own `.edit_existing_regex`, `.move_to_*`, `.export_regex` and `.delete_regex` buttons. A click that these buttons send bubbles to the row, so the row ignores clicks that start inside `[hidden]` (without that guard a test's `dispatchEvent('click')` on `.edit_existing_regex` recursed until the page froze). The row opens the editor on a click, and toggles the selection checkbox instead while bulk edit shows it. The settings normaliser skips the rows through `SB_EXT_AREAS` (`regex_container`, `.regex-script-label`), because it had marked each row a row wrapper and zeroed its metrics. Tests: `tests/unit/extension-row-adapters.test.js`, `tests/audit/surface-audit.e2e.js` (`SURFACE_ID=list.regex-scripts`).
- **Injection positions are select rows:** `adoptPositionRadios(root, name)` (same module) replaces the radios of Summarize (`memory_position`) and Vector Storage (`vectors_position`, `vectors_file_position_db`) with a select that the extension normaliser shows as a select row. The radios stay in the page, hidden, as the state the extension reads and writes. The select reads them once and follows their `input` and `change` events (jQuery's `.trigger` sends no native event, so the adapter listens through jQuery), so Vector Storage calls it after it has set the radios from its settings. The depth and role fields move out of the in-chat radio's label into labelled fields, which the normaliser shows as spin and select rows. These adapters live in a module that already loads at startup, because the startup resource limit in `tests/frontend-performance.e2e.js` (861) counts every request until the page settles, dynamic imports included.
- **A trailing `small` in a switch label is its subtitle:** in the extension pass (`subtitles: 'titled'`), `createRowSubtitle()` takes a trailing `small` inside the label's title span as the row's subtitle, so Quick Reply's "Inject user input automatically (if disabled, use {{input}} ...)" is a title and a subtitle. `#qr--set-settings` is no longer in the Quick Reply `left` area, so its four apply-at-once check boxes are switch rows.
- **Flex columns of rows stack:** an extension's `.flex-container` whose `.flex1` children each hold a row (Vector Storage's query and chunk rows) is a column in the settings page (`sillybunny-tabs.css`), because three rows side by side shrank each to a third of the width.
- **Text areas, sliders and field hints in the extension pass:** `normalizeExtensionSettings()` gives a labelled text area a text-area field (`createTextAreaField`, with the old title's buttons ahead of Expand and the extension's own `.editor_maximize` for that id hidden) and a range a slider row (`adoptExtensionSlider()`). The section pass can pull a bare range into a group and leave its `label for` behind, so a range finds its title by `for` even when it is no longer adjacent; a `title_restorable` class is dropped from the moved title (its bold tracked heading style), a trailing `small` ("0 = default") becomes the subtitle and a restore or auto-adjust button goes to the row's end. A `small` hint between a title and its field, or before the wrapper that holds the field and only its buttons (Regex Presets), becomes the row's caption. `readOnly` text areas and a select without any label are left. Provider forms of Text-to-speech are adopted like any other page now (the `entriesOnly` area is gone), so a provider's selects, switches and sliders are rows. Tests: `tests/unit/extension-rest-adapters.test.js` ("the extension settings pass"), `tests/unit/sillybunny-extension-settings.test.js`.
- **Summarize's loose controls:** `adoptSummarizeSettings()` (same adapter module) builds the summary box as a text-area field with Restore Previous in its label row, the three prompt builders as a toggle row over the same radios (the caption under the title is the chosen builder's tooltip, kept current through jQuery `input` and `change`), and the injection position. `adoptPositionRadios()` keeps an option's explanation (an info icon's title, or its label's tooltip) as `data-sb-description`, which the select row shows under its title while that option is chosen and under the option in the picker (`mapItem`). The Prompt builder radios are a toggle row because there are three of them (DESIGN.md Selection Controls).
- **Option text in brackets is shown without them:** `optionLabel()` in `select-row.js` drops the brackets of an option whose whole text is in them (`[ None ]`, `[ No fallback ]`, `[Currently selected]`) in the row and the picker, and nowhere else. A harness that picks an option by its native text has to apply the same rule (`pickFromPicker()` in `tests/helpers/settings-inventory-steps.js`); without it Expressions' selects "did not restore" in the settings inventory.
- **`hidden` does not hide a node that has a `display` rule of its own:** the Extensions manager's header suffix carries `.ds-row-suffix` (`display: flex`), so `suffix.hidden = true` left the bulk Toggle extensions button visible. With no installed extension the adapter removes the suffix (`tests/extensions-manager-empty.e2e.js`).
- **Character Expressions page (`adoptExpressionsSettings()`, same module):** `addSettings()` in `extensions/expressions/index.js` calls it after the controls have their saved values. It turns the two prompt-strategy radios into a toggle row over the same radios, the agent block's title and two status lines into one row (the status text gets `role="status"`), and the folder override into a floating entry row (help caption under the field, Submit as an icon button at its end) in one boxed group with the seven sprite actions. Each action is the extension's own `<button>` made a button row by `createButtonRow({ button })`, so its id and its click handler stay; the last two (Remove All Sprites, Remove All Image Overrides) are `danger`. Things that broke while building it:
  - **The settings page boxes every visible top-level block of the card into the first group, and leaves a hidden one outside it** unless it shares a tag and a class with the visible block before it (`sharesShape()`). `#open_chat_expressions` is visible when a chat is open, so the whole sprite part (override, buttons, hints, list) landed inside the first group, and the hidden `.expression_prompt_type_block` fell out of the card to sit after it. The wrapper is `display: contents` (it has no box, so the pass skips it and its children are groups of their own), and the prompt-type block carries `m-b-1 m-t-1` like its neighbours. jQuery's `.show()` and `.hide()` on the wrapper still work, because `hide()` sets an inline `display: none`.
  - **`adoptButtonNode()` reads the translation key from the button before the caption:** for a button with `data-i18n="[title]..."` and a caption span with its own key it takes the title key, drops it as no caption key and never reads the span's, so neither translation would reach the row. The adapter removes the title key, builds the row, and puts it back (`tests/unit/expressions-settings-adapter.test.js`, "keeps the translation keys").
  - **A button row on a node that already answers Enter and Space fires twice:** the extension bound `onExpressionGenerationKeydown` to five of its actions (and to the tile's Generate and Regenerate), and the row adds its own key handler. The actions are native `<button>`s now, which answer both keys themselves, and the extension binds no keydown handler.
  - **The tile is not a control:** it was `div.expression_list_item.interactable` holding three `div.menu_button`s, so its buttons sat inside a tab stop. The tile is a `listitem` (`#image_list` is the `list`, named by the sprite-set heading), its picture is a `<button>` (the click bubbles to the tile's delegated handler, which sets the sprite), and Upload, Regenerate, Generate and Delete are native buttons whose tooltip is their name. Over the picture they are overlaid controls (`--sb-osd-bg`, `--sb-osd-ink`). The theme's raised fill is `.menu_button:not(...)` five times, which beats any class selector, so the rule needs `#image_list`.
  - **A select row with two buttons squeezed its value to one letter at 390px** (Custom Expressions showed "N"). The shared rule in `sillybunny-tabs.css` wraps a row at 620px only for three or more buttons; the extension's sheet wraps this row for two. The shared rule is not widened, and other pages with two trailing buttons were not checked.
  - **Header capitalisation changes the visible text only:** the `data-i18n` keys are the upstream ones (a few differ from the text already, as "Re-roll if same expression is used again"), so translations stay. The Lexicon's entry A60 names five of these labels in the old case.
  - **Tests:** `tests/unit/expressions-settings-adapter.test.js`, `tests/unit/sillybunny-button-forms.test.js` (the forms of the page's buttons), `tests/expressions-settings.e2e.js` (empty state, header case, the button rows and the override row, Enter and Space, the sprite list at 1440x900 and 390x844, a character with no sprites).
- **Quick Reply rows and the regex editor:** `adoptQuickReplyRow()` makes each `.qr--set-item` an entity row with a grip, the label and message fields in the text column and one menu button; the icon, options, move, copy, cut, export and remove buttons stay hidden in the row and the menu items click them (a menu click has no Shift, so removing always asks). `QuickReplySet.renderSettings()` sets `role="list"` on the list, because axe requires the parent of a `listitem`. `adaptRegexEditor()` (`extensions/regex/sillybunny-editor.js`) moves the editor's fields into boxed groups; it must not destroy its rows when the editor closes, because `onRegexEditorOpenClick()` reads the fields after the popup resolves (destroying them returned `undefined` for every value, and `tests/window-dialogs.e2e.js` caught it). It loads by dynamic import when an editor opens, so it is not a startup module.

- **A select the extension shows and hides itself needs a row that follows it:** the Translate extension calls `$('#deepl_api_endpoint').toggle(...)`, which sets the select's inline `display`. In a select row the select is a hidden source of truth, so the row stayed on screen with its title while the select was "hidden". `createSelectRow({ followsHidden: true })` hides the row while the select's inline `display` is `none`, and `SB_EXT_TOGGLED_SELECTS` in `sillybunny-settings-presentation.js` lists the selects that take it (the control has to be listed: a select hidden by inline style for another reason keeps its row). `.ds-select-row[hidden]` is in `sillybunny-tabs.css` because a row's `display` beats the `hidden` attribute. A select whose only name is its tooltip takes it as the title through `SB_EXT_TITLED_BY_TOOLTIP`. The Image Generation style select and the Summarize depth and role were already rows at run time; the markup in the settings files only looked unlabelled. Tests: `tests/unit/sillybunny-extension-settings.test.js`.
- **A flex container of select rows and buttons is opened into the group (`adoptWrappedButtonRows()`):** Translate lays its provider select, the DeepL endpoint select and its Key and URL buttons in one `.flex-container`, so the buttons sat loose beside the rows. After the fields are adopted, the container is replaced by its children, and each button gets a button row of its own. The extension shows and hides Key and URL with `.toggle()`, so the row follows the button's inline `display` through a `MutationObserver` on the button. `tests/unit/sillybunny-extension-settings.test.js` holds it.

### Settings sidebar search

- **Breaks:** the old filter matched a page by substring or initials and kept a page only when a heading matched, so `Frequency` found nothing inside a card and `cl` found Console Logs; and a page that was kept showed every row.
- **Files:** `public/scripts/sillybunny-settings-search.js` (matcher, row filter, marks, `createSidebarSearch`); the adapter in `public/scripts/sillybunny-tabs.js` (`createSettingsSearchAdapter`, `getSettingsSearchRoots`, `filterSettingsSidebarTabs`); `.sb-settings-tab-badge`, `.sb-settings-row--filtered` and `::highlight(sb-settings-search-text)` in `public/css/sillybunny-tabs.css`.
- **Guards:**
  - The module loads on demand through `createDeferredModule`, and opening the search field preloads it, so it adds nothing to startup (`tests/frontend-performance.e2e.js`).
  - A typed word has to begin a word of the text and may stop anywhere, so `freq` finds Frequency Penalty and `quency`, `equency` and `temp` (in "attempt") do not; a word that starts with a symbol needs no boundary at its start. A typed `frequencypenalty` matches only a word that starts with it, never the Penalty in it. Text and query are folded alike by `foldSearchText` (lowercase, accents off, one code unit for one code unit so the highlight ranges stay valid; a decomposed accent typed alone is dropped by `normalizeSearchText`). Initials (`cl` for Console Logs) match nothing. Whole-word matching was tried first and dropped on the user's answer to the wave 3 sheet.
  - A row is one child of a top-level `.ds-pref-group` or `.sb-settings-category-body`. It matches when every token is a word of its own text or of its group's title, description or `data-sb-search-aliases`, which is how a group keeps the name it had before a regrouping. Put the alias on the group, never on the page tab.
  - Closed expander and sampler help stay in the row's text, and a match that is only in closed content opens it (`revealClosedMatches`). A row that matches but is not shown (another backend, another device) is not counted and not touched.
  - Rows are hidden by `.sb-settings-row--filtered` and `aria-hidden`, by three repeated classes under `#sb-settings-page` so that no `!important` is needed. `restoreFilteredRows()` runs before each analysis and on clear, so a clear is one synchronous pass. Only the open page is filtered; all pages are counted. Two `MutationObserver`s and a `change` listener on the backend pickers re-run the pass when rows come or go.
  - The Characters pages are only marked and counted; rows are hidden only inside `.sb-wi-settings` and `.sb-persona-settings`, and a lazily mounted panel is indexed once it exists.
  - The reveal waits until the page is shown, and a phone shows the page only after its tab is pressed, so the pulse and scroll run on activation.
  - The count is a bare number in `.sb-settings-tab-badge` (`aria-hidden`) beside a hidden `.sb-settings-tab-count-description` ("1 matching setting", "N matching settings", one `t` string for each), which the tab points at with `aria-describedby`. The tab's name stays its label, so voice control and name-based tests keep working. Both are removed with the count.
  - Neither search may show a blank or wrong state while the on-demand pages load for the first query (`prepareShellSearch`, about 400 ms). The universal search keeps its idle state or its last answer, sets `aria-busy` on the list and replaces the list in one step. The sidebar keeps its tab list and sets `aria-busy` on it, and shows a name hit at once but never the empty message until the pages have answered. Test: "never shows a blank list" and "never shows the empty message" in `tests/sillybunny-settings-search.e2e.js` (they sample every frame; the old code showed 5 to 6 blank frames in the universal list and 5 to 8 empty-message frames in the sidebar).
- **Tests:** `tests/unit/sillybunny-settings-search.test.js`; `tests/sillybunny-settings-search.e2e.js` (clear timing, highlight, reduced motion, aliases, both shells, 1440×900 and 390×844); the alias cases in `tests/unit/sillybunny-settings-content.test.js`; `tests/sillybunny-backend-settings.e2e.js` (page aliases).

### Select rows and the shared picker

- **Breaks:** a select row that replaces select2 loses what select2 showed or did: the details of a model, the placeholder of an empty select, the removal of a saved option that is no longer offered.
- **Files:** `public/scripts/sillybunny-editor/select-row.js`, `public/scripts/sillybunny-editor/picker.js`, `public/scripts/sillybunny-editor/option-details.js`, `public/scripts/sillybunny-model-details.js`, `public/scripts/sillybunny-connections-form.js`.
- **Model details:** the subtitle of a model row (context size, price, capabilities, subscription badge, the Horde model page link) comes from a describer that the provider file registers per select id (`registerOptionDetails()`), applied through `createDetailsMapper()` and the `mapItem` option of `createSelectRow()`. Describers return text only, because the upstream select2 templates in `public/scripts/openai.js`, `public/scripts/textgen-models.js` and `public/scripts/horde.js` build HTML from catalogue fields (they now escape them; see "Security and credentials"). Details pass an allow-list in `option-details.js`: text, `span`, `small`, `i`, and `a` only for an absolute `http` or `https` address without credentials.
- **Placeholders:** select2 is not initialised at 768px and below, so an empty adopted select takes its words from `SELECT2_PLACEHOLDERS` in `sillybunny-connections-form.js` (`pickerNoneLabel()`). A new select2 placeholder needs an entry there; `tests/unit/sillybunny-connections-placeholders.test.js` reads the provider files and fails when an entry is missing or differs.
- **Disabled and selected:** in a picker with `multiple`, a disabled item that is selected can be removed, and a disabled item that is not selected cannot be added (`isItemDisabled()` in `picker.js`). Before that, a saved NanoGPT provider that the model no longer offers could not be deselected.
- **Tests:** `tests/unit/sillybunny-option-details.test.js`, `tests/unit/sillybunny-model-details.test.js`, `tests/unit/sillybunny-picker.test.js`, `tests/unit/sillybunny-select-row.test.js`, `tests/unit/sillybunny-connections-placeholders.test.js`; `tests/nanogpt-controls.e2e.js`, `tests/horde-model-links.e2e.js`.
- **Row click and extension actions:** a click on the row background opens the picker, so the handler must skip every control the row holds. It once skipped only `button, a, input, select, textarea`. When the settings pass adopted the TTS provider forms (`9c5c96573`), SpeechT5's upload and delete actions (`div.menu_button`) became the suffix of the Speaker row; a click on delete opened its destructive alert and then the picker, which took the focus from Cancel. `OWN_CONTROL_SELECTOR` in `select-row.js` now also skips `[role="button"]`, `.menu_button` and `.right_menu_button`, and a handled (`defaultPrevented`) click. Any new row-background handler needs the same list, as `focusOnPress()` in `rows.js` has. Tests: the two cases in `tests/unit/extension-rest-adapters.test.js` (a select row, and the settings pass over SpeechT5's markup); `tests/native-dialog-replacements.e2e.js` ("SpeechT5 speaker deletion…").
- **Fixes:** `55244b488`.
- **Open:** the row dispatches only `change` when a value is picked (`writeValue()` in `select-row.js`), while a native select fires `input` first. `#openrouter_sort_models` listens for `input` (`public/scripts/openai.js`), so a choice made through its row is not saved. `tests/audit/settings-inventory.e2e.js` found it: the fixture records that control as saving nothing.

### Menu and popover surfaces

- **Breaks:** an exclusion written as `:not(.sb-action-menu h3)` on the theme's broad `h3` rule raised that rule's specificity, so it outranked the Connections page's own heading rules (9.4a). A popover that kept its own border, or drew its own ring, showed a doubled edge once `--sb-shadow-popover` gained a ring: 9.3b's type-ahead lists drew a `0 0 0 1px` ring in front of the shadow that already holds one.
- **Files:** `public/css/sillybunny-theme.css` (`--sb-popover-ring`, `--sb-shadow-popover`, `--sb-typeahead-shadow`, the `h3` rule); `public/scripts/sillybunny-action-menu.js`; `public/scripts/sillybunny-autocomplete-clamp.js`; `public/scripts/sillybunny-options-menu.js`; `public/scripts/swipe-picker.js`.
- **Guards:**
  - Exclusions inside a broad element rule go in `:where()`, which adds no specificity.
  - `--sb-shadow-popover` draws the popover edge as a `1px` ring (`--sb-popover-ring`, the border colour under `prefers-contrast: more`). A popover sets `border: 0` and uses that shadow; `--sb-typeahead-shadow` is only an alias of it.
- **The ring has no edge in forced colours (fixed):** forced colours remove box shadows, so a surface whose only edge is `--sb-shadow-popover` or `--sb-typeahead-shadow` had none once menus, sheets and popovers dropped their border (`dialogs-accessibility.e2e.js`, the assistant menu sheet at 390×844: `border none 0px`). One rule in the theme sheet's `@media (forced-colors: active)` block draws a `1px` `CanvasText` outline at `outline-offset: -1px` on every such surface, which adds no size and changes nothing in normal mode; the `:focus-visible` forced-colours rule is important and wins on a focused surface. A new surface that uses the ring needs its selector in that list. A surface that draws a real border (the Chats Archive mention menu) or is a drag copy of a row is left out. Dialog frames and drawers keep a border, so they have no gap. `tests/focus-contrast-css.test.js` fails for any flat rule that uses the ring and is not in the list.
- **Popovers that kept a border (fixed):** the persona and World Info property popovers and the Conversation popover set `border: 0`. The type-ahead rule names `.ui-autocomplete.ui-widget-content`, because jQuery UI's `.ui-widget.ui-widget-content` border (0,2,0) outranked a bare `.ui-autocomplete`. Still bordered under the ring, not named by any task: `.sb-search-results` and the model-id and inline-select picker menus in `sillybunny-tabs.css`, and `.sb-color-picker-menu`.
- **A picker is a sheet in the whole mobile layout:** `openDialog()` and `createDialogFrame()` take `sheetQuery` (default `OVERLAY_SHEET_QUERY`); the colour picker passes `MENU_SHEET_QUERY`. The sheet rules key on the host's `data-presentation`, so only the query moves. Tests: `tests/sillybunny-color-picker.e2e.js` ("opens as a bottom sheet at 768x800"), `tests/sillybunny-mobile-overlays.e2e.js`.
- **Suggestion lists stay inside the window:** `public/scripts/sillybunny-autocomplete-clamp.js` listens to `autocompleteopen`, shifts the list sideways and caps its height, and never flips it above the field. It loads when the first list opens (a one-time listener in `sillybunny-tabs.js`), because a startup module counts against the 861-resource budget of `tests/frontend-performance.e2e.js`. Test: `tests/autocomplete-clamp.e2e.js`.
- **The Options menu leaves out what does not apply:** `public/scripts/sillybunny-options-menu.js` hides CFG Scale unless the backend is a text completion type listed in `#cfg_block_ooba[data-tg-type]` (ooba, tabby) and Token Probabilities unless the backend requests logprobs (text completion, NovelAI, the Chat Completion sources of `logprobsSupportedSources` in `openai.js`; a unit test keeps that list equal). It runs when `#options` is shown, through a style observer in `sillybunny-tabs.js`. Tests: `tests/unit/sillybunny-options-menu.test.js`, `tests/options-menu-applicability.e2e.js`.
- **The swipe picker row ends in one ⋮:** `swipe-picker.js` keeps upstream's four row controls in the row, hidden (`data-sb-forwarded`), and the menu clicks them, so their handlers still run. Delete Swipe is last in its own section and is left out for a swipe that cannot be deleted. Shift+F10 and the Menu key on the row open the menu. Tests: `tests/swipe-picker.e2e.js`, `tests/libadwaita-swipe-styling.test.js`.
- **Tests:** `tests/focus-contrast-css.test.js`; `tests/sillybunny-connections-layout.e2e.js` (headings); the menu family of `tests/audit/surface-audit.e2e.js`.

### Characters and Groups list (list-view parity)

- **Files:** `public/scripts/sillybunny-character-list.js`, `public/css/sillybunny-character-list.css`, `public/css/sillybunny-entity-list.css`, `public/scripts/sillybunny-editor/list-show-more.js`, `public/scripts/sillybunny-editor/tag-filter-menu.js`.
- **The tag picker listed 50 tags (#945):** `attachTagFilterMenu()` builds its options from the `.rm_tag_filter` chips, and upstream's `printTagList()` prints 50 chips and a `.placeholder-expander` chip (`DEFAULT_TAGS_LIMIT` in `public/scripts/tags.js`). With 245 tags the picker offered 50, and a search for a later tag said "no results". Opening the picker now clicks the expander first (`open()` in `tag-filter-menu.js`); the expansion stays until reload, as upstream's does. The expander chip has no `actionable` class, so `TAG_SELECTOR` listed it as a "..." option, and `printTagFilters()` prints with `empty: false`: the expander and the first 50 chips stayed in the list, every later open clicked the stale expander again, and the skipped tags came after it, so an active tag beyond the 50th sat out of order. `TAG_SELECTOR` now skips `.placeholder-expander`, and `showHiddenTags` in `tags.js` removes the expander and the chips of its own print when the list is not emptied (`!empty`), before it prints again. Removing only the expander left the order wrong. The removal goes by ID, not by `.tag:not(.actionable)`, because `printTagList()` is exported and a caller that prints two batches into one list must keep the first batch. Cost, measured with 245 tags: the first open takes about 0.5 s at 1440x900 and 0.7 s at 390x844 (it was 0.05 and 0.13 s), and each filter change redraws 245 chips. Test: `tests/unit/sillybunny-tag-filter-menu.test.js` "expands the upstream chip limit…" (its expander has no `actionable` class and stays in the list, as the real one does) and `tests/unit/sillybunny-tag-filter-expander.test.js` (the real `printTagList()` and menu, 245 tags, an active tag beyond the 50th, a chip from an earlier print that stays), and `tests/character-list.e2e.js` "tag filter picker lists every tag past the 50-chip limit in order…" (65 tags in a browser: the picker lists them all in order with no "..." option, no expander chip is left, and a filter on tag 060 stays selected when the picker opens again; the `tags.js` change is the one this case needs). A multiple picker also kept its scroll position when the query changed (it keeps it for a toggled item, on purpose), so a search typed after scrolling showed the list from the middle; `applyPickerSearch()` in `picker.js` now returns to the top (`tests/unit/sillybunny-picker.test.js` "picker search scroll").
- **A rule in the sheet can hide a tint for months:** `.sb-character-list .ds-entity-row { background: transparent }` (0,2,0) beat `.sb-character-list :where(… > .is-folder-member)` (0,1,0), so the 4% member tint never drew, and a folder header's 8% rest fill (specificity 1,3,1) hid its hover and press fills. Deleting the transparent rule made axe measure the member tint, and the muted caption fell under 4.5:1 on it. The member rule is gone; the folder header's hover and press are `background-image` gradients in `body:not(.charListGrid)`. Tests: `tests/character-list.e2e.js` "a folder header in list view fills on hover and darker on press…".
- **Show More replaces the pager (F-160):** the footer holds one button (`createListShowMore()`); the saved `Characters_PerPage` and `Groups_PerPage` are the batch size, and nothing in the interface writes them. A render that a newer one superseded must not write its batch count (`writeBatches` checks `isCurrent`). Tests: `tests/unit/sillybunny-character-list.test.js` (Show More and batch cases), `tests/character-list.e2e.js` "Show More", `tests/foundation/shell-navigation.e2e.js`.
- **Sort, grid and page size live in the search bar (p6 review):** the sort button is a `controls` child of the list's `createListSearch` field (`.sb-list-search-bar`, shared rules in `sillybunny-editor-shell.css`, as World Info and Agents have it), so the header holds only Search and the menu. A test that opens the sort menu opens the search first (`openHeaderMenu('sort')` in `tests/character-list.e2e.js`, the `character-list.sort` menu in `tests/helpers/surfaces/menu.js`). The header Search toggle and the sort button show `aria-pressed` while the sort is not the default (name, ascending, no rule) or grid is on.
- **The grid cards stretched with the window (resize round):** `repeat(auto-fill, minmax(min(100%, 190px), 1fr))` gave every card the share of the free width, so a column that a wider window added shrank every card, and the cards grew again until the next column (190px to 247px over the 12 widths of the test, measured on the old rule). From 769px a card is now a track of `minmax(min(100%, 190px), 190px)` and the grid has `justify-content: center`, as a GtkFlowBox of fixed-size children with `halign` centre: the cards keep one width, the space left over splits to both sides, and the last row starts at the first column (`public/css/sillybunny-character-list.css`, `body.charListGrid … #rm_print_characters_block`). 190px is the old column minimum and the card's `min-height`; the content alone needs 142px (116px avatar, 24px padding, 2px border), so a smaller card is possible but was not chosen. The phone layout (`@media (pointer: coarse), (max-width: 768px)`) keeps two stretching columns, because a 190px card leaves one column and a gap on a 390px phone. The Groups view shares the rule. A folder header spans `1 / -1`, which now means the width of the tracks, not of the page. Tests: `tests/character-list.e2e.js` ("grid view of the lists": 12 widths from 1100px to 2600px for Characters and Groups, every card 190px, the gaps left and right of the first row equal within 1px, the last row at the first column, no overflow, at least four different column counts; a phone keeps two stretching columns).
- **The Characters search uses `closeEmptyOnBlur` and is driven through the control (p6 follow-ups):** the render restores a view's saved query with `search.open(query, { focus: false, animate: false })` or `search.close({ focus: false, animate: false })` instead of setting `searchField.hidden`. `animate: false` hides the field synchronously, so a blur-close cannot race the view-switch restore, which is what kept the option off before ("favorites, search, sort, page size and grid stay per view" in `tests/character-list.e2e.js`). The option holds a blur-close while a pointer press is inside the bar (Safari does not focus a pressed button), so the agent list's own copy of the blur and pointer code is gone; its list uses the option. A list that opts in must drive the control (`open()`/`close()`), never `hidden`: the World Info editor still restores `searchField.hidden` directly (`sillybunny-world-info-editor.js`, in the render). Tests: `tests/unit/sillybunny-list-search.test.js`, `tests/unit/sillybunny-character-list.test.js`, `tests/character-list.e2e.js` ("an empty search closes when the focus leaves it, and a query or a view switch keeps the field in step").
- **A press inside the field is not a blur-close (Safari):** Safari gives a pressed button no focus, so the input's blur has no `relatedTarget` and an empty field closed under the click on its own sort button. `list-search.js` tracks `pointerInside` from `pointerdown` and skips the close. Test: `tests/unit/sillybunny-list-search.test.js` "waits for the pointer that caused the blur to finish its click".
- **A press elsewhere, after the Sort or Filter menu, closed nothing (p6 review):** `closeEmptyOnBlur` followed only the input's `blur`. A press on the bar's own button moves the focus to it (the blur is ignored), the menu takes it and gives it back to the button on Escape or a chosen item, and the input never blurs again, so a later press elsewhere moved the focus off the button and closed nothing. It affected every bar with a button: the prompt list, World Info, the persona list, the Characters list and the Agents list (Filter). `list-search.js` now listens to `focusout` on the whole field and to `pointerdown` on the document, so a press outside closes an empty field whatever holds the focus, after the click, like a blur close. The rules that keep it honest:
  - A control of the field that reads `aria-expanded="true"` has a menu or popover open (`menuOpen`, kept by a `MutationObserver`); focus in that popup and presses in it are inside. A press outside while it is open closes the menu only, as its light dismiss does, and the next press closes the field. The shared action menu dismisses on a window capture listener, so it has dropped `aria-expanded` before the field's document listener runs; the flag therefore falls one task after the attribute does (`syncMenu`), and a press in the same task is still read as the menu's.
  - A press that is only a press (no blur has moved the focus) is dropped on `pointercancel`: a touch that turns into a scroll is no click, and must not close the bar.
  - The header toggle counts as inside for a press as well as for a blur, so a press on it never queues a close ahead of the toggle's own click (which would close the field and let the click open it again).
  - A test that sorts twice from an open empty bar opens the search again after any press outside it (the prompt list's "a sort other than the prompt order…" does, and `openSearchBar()` in `tests/character-list.e2e.js` reads `data-state`, not `isVisible()`, because a closing field keeps a 2px box for a moment).
  - The audit harness presses and blurs too: `prepareForCapture()` (`tests/helpers/capture-page.js`) keeps the focus on a button inside `.sb-list-search-bar` or `.ica-agents-search`, because blurring the Sort button closed the empty bar a state shows open (World Info `custom-order` and `disabled-only`); `openSearch()` and `clearSearch()` (`tests/helpers/surfaces/list-steps.js`) wait for the field's exit animation first, because a field that closed itself is `hidden` with `data-state="closed"` yet keeps its box while it animates out, so `isVisible()` says yes and the toggle click that follows opens it again.
  - Tests: `tests/unit/sillybunny-list-search.test.js` (`closeEmptyOnBlur`), and `expectSortKeepsSearchDismissable()` (`tests/helpers/list-search-dismiss.js`) in the specs of the four editors and the Agents list. The Tag Manager's frame search and the dialog searches still have no `closeEmptyOnBlur`.
- **Folder collapse animates, expand animates after the render (p6 review):** `createFolderMotion()` in `sillybunny-editor/folder-row.js` plays the member rows' exit (height, padding, margin, border, opacity; `fill: forwards`, rows `inert`) and then commits `setFolderExpanded`; a fallback timer of `MOTION_SLOW + 150` ms commits when a tab does not render and the animation never finishes. Expand commits first and marks the folder pending for 1500 ms; the next render calls `reveal()` after `decorateFolderSections`. Reduced motion, an empty folder and a folder over 60 members commit at once. The Characters list skips `animateListChange` while `folderMotion.pending`, or the two animations fight. The chevron keeps `fa-chevron-down` and turns 180° under `[aria-expanded='true']` (`sillybunny-entity-list.css`, reduced-motion guarded). Tests: `tests/unit/sillybunny-folder-row.test.js`, `tests/character-folders.e2e.js` and `tests/persona-folders.e2e.js` "a folder closes and opens with motion on its rows and a turning chevron, and reduced motion skips both".
- **A folder header's accessible name is its content (WCAG 2.5.3, Label in Name):** `createFolderRow()` in `public/scripts/sillybunny-editor/folder-row.js` no longer sets an `aria-label`. The header is a `role="button"` whose title and count are its text, so its name is the title, a space, then the count (`Cast 2 personas`); the former label `Cast, 2 personas` held a comma that the visible text lacked, and axe `label-content-name-mismatch` recorded it on the Characters and Groups lists. The chevron and the colour dot are `aria-hidden`, so neither is in the name. `entity-row.js` writes `aria-label` on every `update({ label })`, so the folder row removes the attribute again after each update, and the Backgrounds gallery no longer removes it itself. Tests: `tests/unit/sillybunny-folder-row.test.js` ("folder row accessible name"), `tests/character-list.e2e.js` ("folder header is named by its visible title and count", Characters and Groups, open and closed, with an ARIA snapshot), `tests/persona-folders.e2e.js`, `tests/background-folders.e2e.js`. Eight records left `tests/fixtures/axe-baseline.json`.
- **Under load the folder test fails with or without the change:** `tests/character-folders.e2e.js` "Manager create, editor assignment, bulk move…" at 390px timed out in the sheet clicks at a load average above 60, on this tree and on a copy of the base commit alike, and passed alone at a load of 12. From `0b3d2addd` it fails at a low load too: 3 of 3 repeats (`-s 1 -w 3 --repeat-each 3`) on that commit and on the next merge, and a run alone (`-s 1 -w 1`), each timing out at line 194 or 201 (the folder header's collapse, or its saved `expanded` flag); the cause is not known yet.

### Chats Archive, Chat History and Data Bank (list-view parity)

- **Files:** `public/scripts/extensions/sillybunny-chats-archive/` (`src/ui/root.js`, `list.js`, `filters.js`, `dom.js`, `search.js`, `style.css`), `public/scripts/sillybunny-chat-dialogs.js` and the Chat History rules in `public/css/sillybunny-tabs.css`, `public/scripts/sillybunny-editor/adapters/data-bank-dialog.js` and `public/scripts/extensions/attachments/style.css`.
- **A reset on the upstream row hid the shared fills:** `.sb-chat-history-list-page .select_chat_block.ds-entity-row { background: transparent }` (0,3,0) beat `.ds-entity-row--activatable:hover` and `.ds-entity-row.is-selected` (0,2,0), so the open chat and the hovered row computed to `rgba(0, 0, 0, 0)`. The upstream `.select_chat_block:hover` and `[highlight]` fills (0,2,0) lose to the entity sheet by order, so no reset is needed. The shared hover applies under `(hover: hover)` only, while a tap leaves `:hover` on, so the upstream white 30% fill showed on a phone until a `@media (hover: none)` rule cleared it for a row that is not selected.
- **The legacy dimming class:** `.select_chat_block_filename_item` (opacity 0.5) is on the file name and the date, size and count. `decorateRow()` removes it, because the entity row styles its own title and subtitle, and because the parity `own` title selector (`[class*="name" i]`) read the three metadata nodes of each row as titles (18 instances at 12.5px).
- **Chat History search is collapsed:** the frame search needs `collapsed: true`, as the other Windows have it; without it the field was open and focused at the top of every list. `displayPastChats()` in `script.js` focuses the hidden legacy `#select_chat_search` after 200ms, which is left alone; the frame opens its search only when that field already holds a query. Tests: `tests/unit/sillybunny-chat-dialogs.test.js`, `tests/sillybunny-overlays.e2e.js` "Chat History moves its live controls…".
- **One row menu, and the row is a list item:** rename, export (two forms) and delete are one menu on the row, built from the hidden upstream nodes in `.sb-chat-history-legacy-actions`, whose delegated handlers in `script.js` and `bookmarks.js` still run. A row with `role="button"` that holds a button fails axe (`nested-interactive`), so the wrapper is the list item and `#select_chat_div` is a list only while it has rows (the empty page is not a list item).
- **Data Bank rows sit with the upstream template:** `manager.html` keeps the row template (`.attachmentListItemTemplate .attachmentListItem`) in the manager root. A selection mode that selects `.attachmentListItem` there counts it as a row, so Select All checked 29 of 28 attachments; the adapter selects `.attachmentsList .attachmentListItem`. The "No data" row is built by `chats.js` and is marked a list item by the adapter, because its list has `role="list"` (axe `aria-required-children`).
- **Bulk Edit drives the shared selection mode:** the upstream bulk handlers read `.attachmentListItemCheckbox:checked` and clear the checkboxes (and `.attachmentsBulkEditCheckbox`) after an action. The adapter mirrors the selection to those checkboxes, clicks the hidden `.bulkAction*` controls from the shared action bar, and leaves selection mode when `report()` finds the flag cleared. A cancelled action keeps both.
- **The Data Bank Window is a Popup, not a dialog host:** `.sb-dialog-host [hidden] { display: none !important }` does not reach it, so a header button that selection mode hides with `hidden` kept `display: grid` from `.sb-dialog__icon-button`. `attachments/style.css` carries the same rule under `.sb-data-bank-window`. F-073 ("No results" page drawn while hidden) did not reproduce: `.sb-status-page[hidden]` is in `sillybunny-editor-shell.css`; `tests/window-dialogs.e2e.js` asserts the computed display of `.data-bank-empty-search`.
- **Archive banner and status page:** a status page owns the message (`ui.statusPageShown`), so the banner hides while one shows and the loading page carries Cancel. The query strip of the pushed list page is `.sbca-query-strip`; it must not share `.sbca-banner`, which the specs and the registry select (a second match breaks strict locators).
- **Filters popover:** a select pick closes the popover, the Orphan Reason row included. A date or number field's `block-size` loses to the 46px `min-height` of `.text_pole`, so the fields set `min-block-size: 0`; a 180px number field made "Minimum Size (MiB)" wrap and its row 51px against 44px. A spin row there would add two parity entries (the −/+ buttons read as menu items) and shows no field while empty.
- **Parity:** the 43 `list.archive`, `list.chat-history` and `list.databank` entries are gone (list count 200 to 157), and four axe records were removed. The header entries of `header.dialog.archive` stay: its title rule matches `sb-dialog__subtitle`, and the dialog-wide button markup entry belongs to the dialog frames.
- **Tests:** `tests/unit/data-bank-dialog-adapter.test.js` (Bulk Edit), `tests/unit/sillybunny-chat-dialogs.test.js`, `tests/unit/sillybunny-chats-archive-mount.test.js`; `tests/sillybunny-chats-archive.e2e.js` (Tag row, loading page, Show More), `tests/sillybunny-chats-archive-layout.e2e.js` (query strip), `tests/sillybunny-overlays.e2e.js`, `tests/window-dialogs.e2e.js`.

### Tag Manager and Folder Manager (list-view parity)

- **Files:** `public/scripts/sillybunny-tag-manager.js`, `public/css/sillybunny-tag-manager.css`, `public/scripts/sillybunny-folder-manager.js`, `public/css/sillybunny-folder-manager.css`; the empty-list text of `public/scripts/extensions/regex/` and `public/scripts/extensions/quick-reply/`; the swipe picker's delete icon in `public/css/sillybunny-theme.css`.
- **The Tag Manager draws 50 rows at a time (#945, proposal 5):** search filters the whole collection and then shows `batches × 50` (`BATCH_SIZE`); a created or moved tag opens the batch that holds it; a focused or `aria-invalid` row stays mounted while hidden, so an edit in progress survives a refresh; a drag in a partly loaded list keeps the unloaded rows in their order. `refresh()` returns early when `viewFingerprint()` (sorted ids, counts, query, pins, sort mode) is unchanged. With 245 tags the open went from 4,681 DOM nodes to 980, and the longest open task from 274ms to 114ms at 1440 (review `p6-managers`). Tests: `tests/unit/sillybunny-tag-manager.test.js`, `tests/tag-manager.e2e.js`.
- **A static import in a startup module adds a startup module:** importing `list-show-more.js` statically from `sillybunny-tag-manager.js` took the startup count to 864 against the limit of 863 (`tests/frontend-performance.e2e.js`). The manager loads it with `loadTagManagerModules()` when it opens, and a unit test holds that.
- **Gating the tag-strip fit on visibility moved the axe results (#945, proposal 6, dropped):** an IntersectionObserver gate in `entity-row.js` cut the strips fitted per change from about 49 to 11 with no measurable time gain, and changed what axe saw (`list.characters` counts fell, `editor.group` at 390 gained three `aria-roles` records). Run the axe audit before keeping any change to strip fitting.
- **Extension templates are sanitised:** `renderExtensionTemplateAsync()` passes templates through `DOMPurify.sanitize()` (`public/scripts/templates.js`), which drops non-`data-*` custom attributes. The Regex lists' `no-scripts-text` attribute never reached the page, so an empty list was 0px tall with no text (F-097); the attribute is `data-no-scripts-text` now. A custom attribute in extension markup must be `data-*`.
- **An empty-page action shares its name with a header button:** the empty Tag Manager offers New and the empty Folder Manager New Folder, the same accessible names as the header buttons, so a test that clicks the header button scopes to `.sb-dialog__header-end` (`tests/tag-manager.e2e.js`, `tests/character-folders.e2e.js`, `tests/persona-folders.e2e.js`).
- **The pressed fill was hidden on both managers (F-261):** a transparent `!important` background on the row button outranked the shared `:active` fill; it is removed, and `tests/tag-manager.e2e.js` checks the press fill.
- **A flat row button has no edge in forced colours:** moving the managers' row buttons from `menu_button` to the shared `.ds-entity-row__button` (flat, `border: 0`, transparent) left the Tag Manager's row menu and member buttons with no boundary when colours are forced, which `tests/dialogs-accessibility.e2e.js` ("forced colours: buttons, switches and the focus ring stay visible") reports. `sillybunny-entity-list.css` gives every shared row button a `1px ButtonBorder` outline under `(forced-colors: active)` (`449ecc8e7`). The task's related run did not include that spec, because `ds-entity-row__button` is a shared name; the wave's related run caught it.
- **The row subtitle and the search placeholder use the shared muted inks:** `.sb-tag-manager .ds-entity-row__subtitle` and `#tag_view_list .text_pole::placeholder` had body ink; the subtitle rule is deleted, so the shared `.ds-entity-row__subtitle` (`--sb-muted-fg`) applies, and the placeholder rule stays with `--sb-input-placeholder`, because upstream's `.text_pole::placeholder { color: var(--SmartThemeEmColor) !important }` (`public/style.css`) beats the theme's non-important `input.text_pole::placeholder`; a placeholder of a `.text_pole` needs its own `!important` rule, as the other lists have. Read from the painted pixels: subtitle 5.0:1 on the dark tone and 5.55:1 on the light one, placeholder 6.33:1 and 5.36:1. Axe cannot decide the contrast of most of this list (172 node-runs of `list.tag-manager` are "undecided" before and after), so it showed no record either way. Tests: `tests/characters-menu-contrast-css.test.js`, `tests/tag-manager.e2e.js` ("take the shared muted inks").
- **Both managers have a selection mode, with Delete only (p6 review):** the Tag Manager enters it from "Select" in its menu, the Folder Manager from a new menu with one "Select", right-click or long-press. Bulk delete confirms once with `confirmDestructiveAction` (text is `TODO(copy)` plus the count). Tags delete through `removeTagById()` and `deleteTagsById()` in `tags.js` (the row Delete uses the same core; the adapter action is `actions.deleteMany`), folders through `deleteFolder(scope, id)` per folder, and their members stay. In a dialog frame the selection `className` lands on the surface itself, so the selector is `.sb-tag-manager[data-selection-mode='true']`, the cancel button is `.sb-dialog__selection-cancel`, and there is no `.sb-selection-header`. Tests: `tests/tag-manager.e2e.js` "selects tags, keeps the rows' own controls out of use and deletes the selection", `tests/unit/sillybunny-tag-manager.test.js`, `tests/unit/sillybunny-folder-manager.test.js`.
- **The Tag Manager's sort is in its search bar:** the dialog frame's `search.controls` slot (`sillybunny-editor/dialog.js`) holds the sort button; `tag_sort_mode !== 'manual'` shows it pressed. The frame search closes an empty search when focus or a press leaves it only for a caller that sets `search.closeEmptyOnBlur` (`sillybunny-editor/dialog.js`), which the Tag Manager does since 9.4a; a test that calls `blur()` on that field now closes it, so `tests/helpers/dialog-entry-parity.js` moves focus to a sibling control instead.
- **The Folder Manager rows take the shared entity-row semantics, and `list.folder-manager` has a `selection-mode` audit state (p6 follow-ups):** the rows had `role="button"` over a nested input, which gave axe `aria-required-children`, `label-content-name-mismatch` and `nested-interactive` records. `createEntityRow` already gives an activation-`navigate` row `role="listitem"` and `tabIndex=0`, so `sillybunny-folder-manager.js` no longer sets either; the row keeps `aria-haspopup="dialog"`. Nine records left `tests/fixtures/axe-baseline.json`, and the state (menu "Select", two `.sb-selection-check`, the selection-bar delete) mirrors the Tag Manager's in `tests/helpers/surfaces/list.js`. The Tag Manager's own `row.setAttribute('role', 'button')` (`sillybunny-tag-manager.js`, near the row build) is dead: `a11y.js` rewrites the role to `listitem` when it observes the row, which is why the Tag Manager never had these records.
- **`menu.tag-manager.header` fails its axe check at the base:** two `aria-required-children` records (`div.sb-action-popover > div#sb-action-menu` at 1440x900, the `.sb-action-sheet` form at 390x844) are in `tests/fixtures/axe-baseline.json` but no longer found, so the check reports them as stale. Remove them with `SURFACE_AXE_UPDATE=1` in the task that owns the Tag Manager menu.
- **Swipe picker delete icon spun on hover (F-111):** upstream's `.PastChat_cross:not(.disabled):hover` runs `infinite-spinning`; `.swipe_picker_block .swipe_picker_delete:not(.disabled) { animation: none; }` stops it without a `:hover` selector. Test: `tests/swipe-picker.e2e.js` (fails without the rule).

### Persona list, folders and header

- **Files:** `public/scripts/sillybunny-persona-editor.js` (list), `sillybunny-persona-detail.js` (editor), `sillybunny-folders.js` (store), `sillybunny-folder-manager.js` (Manager adapter), `public/css/sillybunny-persona-editor.css`, `sillybunny-persona-detail.css`, `sillybunny-entity-list.css`, `sillybunny-folder-manager.css`.
- **Persona folders are a third scope of `power_user.sb_folders`:** `personas: { folders: [{ id, name, color, expanded }], members: { <avatarId>: <folderId> } }`, one folder per persona, the same shape as `characters` and `groups`. The scope is lazy: `normalizeFoldersData()` keeps `personas` only when the source has it, and the writers create it on the first write (`writableScope()`), so settings saved before persona folders load and save back byte for byte. A persona's key is its avatar id, so a rename needs nothing. `PERSONA_CREATED` with `duplicatedFromAvatarId` copies the membership (a duplicate keeps its folder) and `PERSONA_DELETED` removes it; `power-user.js` configures the store before the editor subscribes, so the copy exists when the list refreshes. Tests: `tests/unit/sillybunny-folders.test.js` (persona scope cases), `tests/persona-folders.e2e.js`.
- **No scope prunes memberships at load, so the Folder Manager counts only members that exist:** `normalizeMembers()` in `sillybunny-folders.js` drops keys whose folder is gone, never keys whose character, group or persona is gone, and rewriting stored data at load would also touch the characters and groups scopes. A persona removed outside `deletePersonaRecord` (its avatar file deleted on disk, a restore that drops it, another browser) leaves a stale `members` key. The persona and character lists build their counts from the entities they hold, so they were right; the Manager's row count used `getFolderMembers(...).length` and was one too high per stale key (its members page already filtered). `liveMemberCount()` in `sillybunny-folder-manager.js` now counts only keys the adapter still lists, for every scope; the stale key stays in settings and counts again if the entity returns. Tests: `tests/unit/sillybunny-folder-manager.test.js` (persona scope, "leaves out members that no longer exist"), `tests/unit/sillybunny-folders.test.js` ("loading keeps a membership…", "renaming a persona keeps its folder…"), `tests/persona-folders.e2e.js` ("removed outside the app", and the members page add and remove).
- **The Folder Manager's persona wording is `TODO(copy)`, marked in markup:** its delete alert goes through `openAlert`, which has no placeholder option, so `confirmDelete()` passes a `bodyNode` carrying `data-sb-copy-placeholder="true"` when the body is the placeholder (the bulk delete uses `confirmDestructiveAction`'s `copyPlaceholder`). No test reaches the persona backup payload (`onBackupPersonas` in `personas.js`: `personas`, `persona_descriptions`, `default_persona`); folders are not in it, by design.
