- **A filled copy slot loses its marker with it:** when a placeholder gets a real string, remove that site's `data-sb-copy-placeholder` and `data-sb-copy-slot` too, and keep the marker on a part that is still open (the Recent Chats error page keeps `HOME-RECENT-FAILED-DESCRIPTION` on its description while the title is `Chats Not Loaded`; Help's no-results page keeps `HELP-SEARCH-EMPTY-BODY`). `tests/home-windows.e2e.js` (`expectSlots` for Help's no-match page), `tests/home-layout.e2e.js` (the failed Recent Chats page), `tests/home-assistants.e2e.js` and `tests/unit/sillybunny-assistants-view.test.js` (the `Unavailable` subtitle carries no marker), `tests/sillybunny-settings-consistency.e2e.js` (the `Thumbnail Actions` heading) and `tests/unit/sillybunny-fonts-page.test.js` (the font toasts) pin both sides.
- **The Folder field is an immediate state action, not a draft field:** like the lorebook link and the Default switch it applies through `setFolder('personas', …)` at once, so it is not in `DRAFT_KEYS`, never dirties the editor and survives Cancel. It is a `createSelectRow` adopting a `<select>` (the shared row, not `createComboRow`). A select row's base row has no control, so `setDisabled()` only toggles `inert` and `aria-disabled`: set the select's own `disabled`, then call `sync()` (the row's MutationObserver syncs later). In tests choose the folder through the trigger and the picker's option, because the native select is hidden.
- **Show More replaces the persona pager (F-160):** the batch size is `Personas_PerPage` when it is a positive integer, otherwise 50; a search or sort change resets the batch, and `selectPersona()` reveals the batch that holds the persona and opens a collapsed folder. `createListPagination` stays for World Info.
- **A persona's summary button showed at 1440 (F-301):** `#PersonaManagement .sb-persona-editor .menu_button:where(…)` (1,2,0) in `sillybunny-persona-editor.css` beat the later `.sb-persona-entry .sb-persona-property-summary { display: none }`, so the summary showed beside the property bar. The hide rule now carries `.sb-persona-editor:not([data-collapsed='true'])`, which lifts it above the button rule whichever sheet loads last. Test: `tests/persona-editor.e2e.js` "a desktop editor shows the property bar and keeps the summary…".
- **The header at 320px (F-063):** the persona header is a `.sb-editor-header` with the shared gap and padding of 6px (scoped under `.sb-persona-editor`, because the agent editor reuses `.sb-persona-entry-header`). The Edit/Preview button shows only while the detail column is 330px wide or more; below that the header cannot hold Back, the name, Preview, the menu, Cancel and Save, so Persona Actions carries an Edit or Preview item (it is added when both header controls compute to `display: none`). Query the detail container (`sb-list-editor-detail`), not the header: a container's inline size is its content box, so the header's own width is 12px less than the column, and the same page measured 344px in a scratch run and 334px in `tests/in-chat-agents-editor.e2e.js`. Below 300px the name's minimum is 40px. Tests: `tests/persona-editor.e2e.js` "the editor header holds Save and the menu at 320px…", `tests/persona-management-ui.e2e.js` (`showPreview()` takes the menu path below 376px viewport width).
- **The shared row is 12px:** `.ds-entity-row` takes `padding: 6px 12px` (`sillybunny-entity-list.css`). A list that restates its own inline padding keeps it (the tag manager's `.tag_view_item { padding-inline: 8px }` stays at 8px; the Characters list restates the same 12px); the folder manager's 8px restate was removed. The agent list's title moved 4px right at 1440 (`tests/in-chat-agents-list.e2e.js` `selectionLayout`). An axe record keys on the selector path, so a wrapper added to the persona list (`.ds-entity-list__rows`) changed the path of its one `row-menu` contrast record; it was edited in `tests/fixtures/axe-baseline.json` in place, not added.
- **The persona list has the shared selection mode and its sort in the search bar (p6 review):** entry is right-click, long-press or "Select" in the list menu. Actions are Move to Folder (`setFolder`), Duplicate and Delete. Delete confirms once, then calls `deletePersonaRecord(id, { silent: true })` per persona and replays the default-persona and locked-persona warnings from the `personas.js` strings. The persona search uses `closeEmptyOnBlur`. The header Search toggle and the sort button show `aria-pressed` while `persona_sort_order !== 'asc'`. The selection header takes the shared `.sb-selection-header-host` rule, `padding: 6px 9px; gap: 3px`, which started as the persona value (on-scale) and is now every list's (see the shared-rule note in the World Info section). Test: `tests/persona-editor.e2e.js` "Sort ends the search bar, and Select files the selected personas into a folder", the `selection-mode` audit state of `list.persona`.
- **Tests:** `tests/unit/sillybunny-persona-editor.test.js`, `tests/unit/sillybunny-persona-detail.test.js`, `tests/unit/sillybunny-folders.test.js`; `tests/persona-editor.e2e.js`, `tests/persona-folders.e2e.js`, `tests/persona-management-ui.e2e.js`.

### Backgrounds gallery and folders (list-view parity)

- **Files:** `public/scripts/sillybunny-background-gallery.js` (sections, header, menus, selection, long press), `sillybunny-background-sections.js` (the section model, no DOM), `public/css/sillybunny-background-gallery.css`, `sillybunny-folders.js` (the `backgrounds` scope), `sillybunny-folder-manager.js` (its adapter hooks) and thin marked calls in `backgrounds.js`, `index.html`, `slash-commands.js` and `SlashCommandCommonEnumsProvider.js`.
- **Folders and memberships stay upstream's, in the image-metadata index:** `power_user.sb_folders.backgrounds` holds only `{ id, name, color, expanded }` records for order, colour and expanded state; its `members` stay empty. `getFolders('backgrounds')` joins those records with the index (`mergeBackgroundFolders()`): the index decides which folders exist and their names, a record whose id the index lost is skipped and not rewritten at load, and an indexed folder with no record shows with the defaults. Writing one folder's state writes the whole merged list, so the order the user sees is kept. A folder created in the manager takes the id of `/api/image-metadata/folders/create` first (`createFolder(..., { id })` throws without it). Tests: `tests/unit/sillybunny-folders.test.js` (Backgrounds scope), `tests/unit/sillybunny-background-sections.test.js`, `tests/unit/sillybunny-folder-manager.test.js` (backgrounds scope).
- **One folder per file in the interface, several in the data:** Move to Folder unassigns a file from every folder it was in and assigns the chosen one (`bridge.move`); a file that upstream data placed in two folders shows in both until it is moved, and the Folder Manager's Remove takes it out of that folder only (`removeFromFolder()`). The bridge queues its requests so a move and a rename reach the index in order, and a failed request reloads the index.
- **A closed folder builds no thumbnails, so nothing may read the DOM for the list of backgrounds:** `/bg`, its enum and Auto-select read `getBackgroundTargets()` and the cached server list (`backgrounds.js`), not `.bg_example` nodes. A delete that finds no thumbnail picks the replacement from the cache, and never the hidden `#background_template` node. `highlightNewBackground()` returns when the file has no thumbnail.
- **The gallery module loads with the Appearance page, and the page waits for it:** `sillybunny-tabs.js` awaits `initBackgroundGallery()` before `aria-busy` is cleared. Before, the header was rebuilt after the page showed, the list moved, and the surface audit's first thumbnail left the viewport (once in the runs before the page waited). `sillybunny-folders.js` loads at startup, so `mergeBackgroundFolders()` lives there: a separate module for it made the startup resource count 864 against the limit of 863.
- **The tab row ends where the grid ends:** the scroll-to-top button kept its width and gap when it was hidden (`visibility: hidden`), which left the empty space at the end of the row. It leads the buttons now and is out of the layout until it shows. The panel's own border and any classic scrollbar also end the grid before the row, so the gallery measures that distance into `--sb-bg-grid-inset`. Test: `tests/background-folders.e2e.js` (the tab row's end against the grid's end, with and without folders, at 1440 and 390).
- **Touch: no button over the picture, a tap sets, a long press opens the menu:** `.sb-bg-cell-menu` stays in the document and shows only while it has focus (`pointer-events: none` otherwise). Selection mode starts its own selection from a touch long press and from `contextmenu`, so the gallery's capture-phase `pointerdown` marks a touch press and `canEnter` refuses while it lasts; its `contextmenu` and `keydown` listeners also keep Shift+F10 and the Menu key, which browsers answer with a `contextmenu` event, from starting a selection. A right-click with a mouse still starts one.
- **Selection and delete:** Select is in the gallery's ⋮ menu, which the Chat tab hides. A file in two folders is one selection (the key is the file name). The check needs `rowLabel`, or axe reports `label` for every check. Bulk Delete confirms once with the count and calls the single delete's `removeBackground()` per file, with one redraw at the end; `delBackground()` now returns whether the server accepted the deletion, and a refusal keeps the thumbnail and shows a toast.
- **Fixtures:** the settings inventory section of Appearance was recorded again (the gallery's buttons changed; `#bg-sort` and `#auto_background` stay in the document, hidden), `list.backgrounds` has a `selection-mode` state, and six axe records of the removed `.mobile-only-menu-toggle` on global thumbnails left `tests/fixtures/axe-baseline.json`.
- **A section body opens and closes with the member-row motion (13.7c):** `setSectionExpanded()` played a height and opacity animation on the body with the right duration and easing, but the body is `box-sizing: border-box` with 9px padding and a 1px border, so `height: 0px` could not go below 19px: the last third of a close and the first fifth of an open were a 19px box fading in place, and the box snapped by 19px when it was hidden or shown. A click in the middle of a motion restarted from the full or the empty height, and an empty folder (a note, no thumbnails) had no motion at all. `animateFolderBody()` (`sillybunny-editor/folder-row.js`, next to `createFolderMotion`) animates the padding and border to 0 with the height, starts an interrupted motion from the height, padding and opacity on screen, and measures the settled height after cancelling the running motion; it uses the defaults of `play()`, so duration and easing are the member rows'. The folder-row unit test mocks `sillybunny-motion.js` with `MOTION_SLOW`, `play` and `prefersReducedMotion` only, so the helper imports nothing else from it. Measured, a Chromium run showed the animation present before the change, so "no animation" was the snap and the empty folder. Tests: `tests/background-folders.e2e.js` ("a folder opens and closes with the motion of the other folders…", "an empty folder moves its note the same way…", "reduced motion opens and closes a folder with no animation"), `tests/unit/sillybunny-folder-body-motion.test.js`.

### Editor header, token readout and leave dialog

- **Breaks:** each explicit-save editor kept its own copy of the header order, the dirty mark, the Cancel icon form, the count and the leave dialog, so the five differed: the group of Back, Cancel and Save moved between editors, three leave dialogs put Discard in the Cancel slot, and a list editor's count sat in a footer.
- **Files:** `public/scripts/sillybunny-editor/chrome.js` (`mountEditorChrome` orders the header, gives the tooltips and owns `setTokens()`; `createDirtyMark()`, `createTokenReadout()`, `createSummaryRow()`, `attachToLabelRow()`), `sillybunny-editor/leave-dialog.js` (`askLeaveEditor()`, used by `navigation.js` and `list-shell.js`), and the rules in `public/css/sillybunny-character-editor.css`, a deferred sheet (see Cache, boot and reload: "The editors' shared sheet is deferred").
- **Guards:**
  - A control reaches a shared rule only through the shared classes (`sb-editor-icon-button sb-header-icon-button` on Back and the item menu); the editors' `.menu_button:not(.sb-header-icon-button)` rules would otherwise reset it.
  - The theme sets `margin: 0 !important` on an icon `.menu_button`, so an auto margin on the Expand button never applies. The label row gives the heading `flex: 1 1 auto` instead.
  - The popup dialog stacks its buttons (`column-reverse`): the order the theme sets (Cancel -1, Discard 0, Save 1) shows as Save on top and Cancel at the bottom. A test reads the computed `order`, not the box positions.
  - A count that sits in a second row is a second node (`role="group"` with the full text as its name and an `aria-hidden` short value), and CSS shows one of the two; `setTokens()` writes both and reruns the toolbar overflow pass.
  - `tests/frontend-performance.e2e.js` counts startup requests with no headroom (`STARTUP_RESOURCE_LIMIT`); a new module that startup imports needs the limit raised on purpose.
  - The Prompt editor (`public/scripts/sillybunny-prompt-editor.js`) rebuilds its whole detail on every render (a change of mode, Reset to Default, a live reload), so it calls `chrome.dispose()` before it mounts the chrome again, and keeps what must outlive a render (the draft, the counted total, the dirty state) outside the DOM. Its header carries `sb-editor-header sb-prompt-detail-header`, so the shared header rules (gap, padding, the `sb-editor-header` container and the compact segmented tray height in `sillybunny-character-editor.css`) apply to it; it carries neither `.sb-wi-entry-header` nor `.sb-persona-entry-header`. `sillybunny-prompt-editor.css` hides the header count while the pane is collapsed. A pane that has no property summary still gets the summary row, for the count alone.
  - The Prompt editor draft lives in `createEditorSession` and the shared store, key `sb-prompt-editor-draft-<user>-<kind>:<identifier>` (a new prompt is `<kind>:new`, because its identifier does not survive a reload). The payload carries the preset scope and the JSON of the saved prompt, and a backup that no longer matches either is dropped without an offer. Save, Cancel, Discard from the leave dialog and closing a clean draft remove it; the five-second interval and `pagehide` write it. `markSaved()` re-keys a new prompt to its identifier.
  - The header item menu is opened by the chrome, not by the editor's `menu()` helper, so the editor marks the menu `data-sb-prompt-menu` after the click; `sillybunny-preset-autosave.js` takes that mark as part of the editing surface, and a menu without it would not trigger an autosave after Delete or Remove From List.
- **Tests:** `tests/unit/sillybunny-editor-chrome-navigation.test.js`, `tests/unit/sillybunny-editor-leave-dialog.test.js`, `tests/unit/sillybunny-world-info-entry.test.js`, `tests/unit/sillybunny-persona-detail.test.js`, `tests/unit/sillybunny-prompt-editor.test.js` (header order, item menu, count placement, draft); `tests/persona-editor.e2e.js`, `tests/world-info-editor.e2e.js`, `tests/in-chat-agents-editor.e2e.js`, `tests/prompt-manager-characterization.e2e.js`.

### Character and group editor sheet (single-content)

- **Breaks:** the sheet kept stacked `.ds-field` blocks that no other page uses, its header and toolbar changed layout at two unrelated conditions (a 620px viewport query for the count, a 440px container query for Cancel), a group member row carried four controls, and the quick-edit capture opened with the header scrolled away.
- **Files:** `public/scripts/sillybunny-editor/rows.js` (`adoptEditorFields` turns each labelled single-line field into a floating entry row and each text area into a text-area field, in place, keeping the control, its id, name, classes and listeners; the field's token count, Edit/Preview toggle and Expand move into the row), `sillybunny-character-editor.js`, `sillybunny-group-editor.js` (both call it before the chrome mounts), `public/css/sillybunny-character-editor.css`, `public/css/sillybunny-entity-list.css` (member rows), `public/scripts/group-chats.js` (`getGroupCharacterBlock`, `syncGroupListEmptyPage`).
- **Guards:**
  - One header condition: `@container sb-editor-header (width < 560px)` moves the single-content editors' count to the toolbar row and gives every editor's Cancel its icon form. A viewport query would disagree with the sheet's own width.
  - The token count of the Name field is the `[data-token-counter]` span itself, moved into the row's suffix; `.extension_token_counter` (`width: 100%`, right aligned) must not be moved with it.
  - The wrapper keeps its `.ds-field` class and id: `mountEditorPreviews` finds the field through `textarea.closest('.ds-field')`, and the axe baseline records elements by their class path, so a marker class on the wrapper re-keys every record under it. The row styles select the wrapper with `:has(> .ds-row-entry)`.
  - A class with `name` or `title` in it (a pencil called `…-rename-icon`) is read by the header and list parity parts as a title; the rename pencil is `sb-editor-pencil`.
  - The Folder select is filled once at mount, and the folder of the opened character can arrive later (a folder store that loads after the editor mounts): `_setFolderDraft` rebuilds the options first, or the select shows nothing.
  - A 44px touch target on a tag chip's remove button is the button's own box with a negative margin and `background-clip: content-box`; a taller button stacks the chips and pushes Essentials past the screen.
  - `tags.css` dims `#tagList .tag` to 0.6, which fails the contrast rule on the light tone; the editor's chip rule sets `opacity: 1`.
  - A member row opens its character on press (`activation: 'activate'`, no chevron); the same route is the "View character card" item of its menu, so the action keeps a visible form.
  - The quick-edit capture step waits for the tag chips, then scrolls to the end: whether the chips had rendered decided how far the sheet scrolled.
  - The header and toolbar stay in view while the sheet scrolls, on a phone too, as libadwaita's do; the scroll-hide of `mountEditorChrome` and its `sb-editor-chrome-hidden` rules are gone (`character-editor-rendering.e2e.js`, "the header and toolbar stay in view while the sheet scrolls").
  - The "No Character Selected" page has one action, Browse Characters; the New action of the Characters list header creates a character (`mobile-character-toggle-and-panels.e2e.js`).
  - Created by and Character Version are textareas upstream, and a card may hold a line break in them; an `<input>` would drop it on display and on save. They are entry rows that keep their textarea (`adoptEditorFields(root, { entryTextAreas })`, `rows = 1`, `field-sizing: content`), and the Metadata notes start at two field heights and grow. `style.css` gives every `#form_create textarea` `min-height: 20dvh` below 1000px, and the old mobile-shell rules fixed the two at 44px and the notes at 9rem with `!important`; the entry-row textarea needs an id-scoped rule, and the mobile rules are gone (`character-editor-rendering.e2e.js`, "Metadata fields take the size of their content").
  - Tags (task 5.6, the user's picks in `review/p5-tags-prototype/` and `review/p5-tags-field/`): `adoptTagField` (`sillybunny-editor/tag-filter-menu.js`) draws each editor's tags as a select row over a hidden `<select multiple>` of every tag (the names are the row's subtitle and the trigger shows their count, `summarize` in `createSelectRow`: names in the trigger ran on from the title with no break, the user's note), and drives upstream for every change: a tick or a created tag goes through the add field's own jQuery UI `select` handler (`selectTag`, which creates a missing tag and handles a character still being created), an untick clicks the chip's Remove button. The chips (`#tagList`, `#groupTagList`) stay the source of truth, hidden; upstream's `printTagList` stops at 50 chips unless the list has `tags-expanded` (#945), which the field adds. Upstream reprints after an add and opens its own menu from the cleared field, so the field closes it. `SillyTavern.getContext()` throws (`Cannot access 'chat' before initialization`) while the editors mount at start-up, which left the group editor half mounted: the field reads the tags lazily and catches it. The picker's `create` option (`picker.js`) offers the typed words when no item has that label. Tests: `tests/unit/sillybunny-tag-filter-menu.test.js` ("editor tag field"), `tests/unit/sillybunny-picker.test.js`, `tests/character-create-flow.e2e.js`, `tests/character-list.e2e.js`, the `menu.picker.character-tags` audit surface (it replaces `menu.autocomplete.tag-input`).
  - At phone width `sillybunny-mobile-shell.css` gives every `label` in the open right-nav panel `pointer-events: auto`, which outranks the floating title's `pointer-events: none`, so the title of an empty entry row took the taps meant for its field; the rule skips `.ds-row-entry__title` (the `menu.autocomplete.tag-input` audit surface clicks `#tagInput` at 390x844).
  - `openTab('characters', 'editor')` shows the Editor page on a phone, as the other Characters tabs do; the audit step `openAuditCharacterEditor` names the page after it selects the character, because the pane that is off screen is inert.
  - Quick Edit (`power_user.characterEditorQuickEdit`) and Spoiler Free Mode (`power_user.spoiler_free_mode`, the upstream key) are separate: each only toggles a class on `#form_create` (`sb-editor-sheet--quick-edit`, `sb-editor-sheet--spoiler-free`), their hidden sets combine, and neither handler reads the other's setting. Quick Edit shows Essentials, Character Fields, the first message and Metadata. Spoiler Free Mode hides Character Fields, Advanced Fields and Greetings behind the `#sb_character_spoiler_reveal` row; `createDefinitionsReveal` (`sillybunny-editor/rows.js`) keeps the reveal per character, and a new module for it fails the startup resource limit in `tests/frontend-performance.e2e.js`. Specs: `tests/character-editor-modes.e2e.js`, `tests/character-editor-quick-edit.test.js`, `tests/unit/sillybunny-definitions-reveal.test.js`.
- **Parity rules:** the editor-family part `entry` does not measure a floating entry row's input, because the row is the field (`tests/helpers/parity/editor.js`); the list-family row rule holds `DESIGN.md`'s 12px inline padding, and its title selector skips tag chips (`tests/helpers/parity/list.js`).
- **Tests:** `tests/unit/sillybunny-editor-rows.test.js` (`adoptEditorFields`), `tests/unit/sillybunny-group-list-adapters.test.js`, `tests/group-editor-member-lists.e2e.js`, `tests/character-editor-rendering.e2e.js`, `tests/character-list.e2e.js` (tag removal size), the audit specs for `editor.character`, `editor.group`, `list.group-members`, `status.group.members-empty`.

### World Info editor (list-content editor)

- **Breaks:** the list, its header and the entry and settings pages kept their own geometry, resting rules that named an id outranked the shared hover and pressed fills, and a coarse-pointer `min-height: 44px` on `input[type=range]` fattened the shared 4px trough (F-129).
- **Files:** `public/scripts/sillybunny-world-info-editor.js`, `sillybunny-world-info-entry.js`, `sillybunny-world-info-settings.js`, `public/css/sillybunny-world-info-editor.css`; the shared list header (`sillybunny-editor/list-header.js`: `titleButton`, `setSubtitleHidden`) and its rules in `sillybunny-editor-shell.css`.
- **Guards:**
  - A resting rule that names an id beats a shared `:hover` or `:active` rule, so the pressed fill never shows: lower the resting rule with `:where()`.
  - The list-header title, Caption subtitle and spacer are shared rules in `sillybunny-editor-shell.css`; a list editor keeps only its geometry, or a scoped copy holds an old size (the prompt list kept `0.75 × --mainFontSize` until the 6.1 merge).
  - A class substring selector for a title (`[class*="title" i]`) also matches `__subtitle`, so the parity `own` title entries of the list headers read the subtitle's size; the header and status rules now exclude `subtitle` (see "Audit specs, the surface registry and ratchets").
  - **The book switcher is named by what it shows first (axe `label-content-name-mismatch`, Label in Name):** the rule wants all the visible text of a button inside its name, and the button shows the book name and the entry counts, so `nameTitleButton()` in `list-header.js` names it `<title> <subtitle>, <label>` ("Book 3 entries · 3 active, Worlds/Lorebooks") and keeps it current through `setTitle`, `setSubtitle` and `setSubtitleHidden`; with no book open it reads "--- Pick to Edit ---, Worlds/Lorebooks". The two spans are blocks, which axe reads without a space ("Book3 entries"), so a title-button header puts a space text node between them. The old record in `tests/fixtures/axe-baseline.json` is gone. Tests: `tests/unit/sillybunny-list-header.test.js`, `tests/unit/sillybunny-world-info-editor.test.js`, `tests/world-info-editor.e2e.js` ("the book switcher is named…").
  - **Sort and the view filter sit in the search bar:** `.sb-wi-sort-button` is the last child of `.sb-wi-search-field` (the wrapper that `createListSearch` reveals; the entry inside is `.sb-wi-search-entry`), as the agent list's Filter button is, so the header holds the book switcher, Search, New Entry and the book menu and the title gets 50px more at 390px. The Search toggle and the sort button show `aria-pressed` while the sort is not Priority (the saved choice stands in for a search's temporary Search sort) or a view filter other than All is on (`viewAltered()`, read in `renderList()`). `createListSearch({ closeEmptyOnBlur: true })` closes an empty bar when the focus goes outside it, and waits for a pointer press to end first; focus on the sort button stays inside the bar. A spec or registry step that opens the sort menu opens the search first (`openSortMenu()` in `tests/world-info-editor.e2e.js`, `chooseWorldInfoView()` in `tests/helpers/surfaces/list.js`). The sort button's label is still the `lorum ipsum` placeholder.
  - The World Info empty page has one action; New and Import sit in the book picker's footer and Global Settings first in the book menu.
- **The search-bar rules are shared:** `.sb-list-search-bar` (the sort button beside the field) moved from `sillybunny-world-info-editor.css` to `sillybunny-editor-shell.css` in the p6 review, because the persona list, the Characters list and the Tag Manager use it now.
- **One selection-header rule (p6 follow-ups):** `createSelectionMode()` (`sillybunny-editor/selection-mode.js`) puts the class `sb-selection-header-host` on its standalone header, and `sillybunny-editor-shell.css` holds the one rule set: `padding: 6px 9px`, inner `gap: 3px`, inner `min-height` 60px (48px compact), title centred. The Characters, World Info, Prompt, Persona and Agents sheets lost their copies; the `.sb-*-selection-header` classes stay in the DOM because specs select them. Visible change: Characters, World Info and Agents moved 1px (padding 8 to 9, gap 2 to 3); the prompt list's header moved from padding 10 and gap 8 to the shared values, gained the 48px inner minimum, and its count is centred instead of start-aligned (DESIGN.md puts the count in the middle). `spacing-off-scale` fell 1098 to 1091. Tests: `tests/selection-header-css.test.js` (no sheet but the shell styles the host), `tests/unit/sillybunny-selection-mode.test.js` (the host class).
- **Tests:** `tests/world-info-editor.e2e.js`, `tests/unit/sillybunny-world-info-*.test.js`, the audit surfaces `worldinfo|world-info|lorebook`.

### Prompt editor (list-content editor)

- **Breaks:** the Prompt Manager list kept its own header, `.menu_button` row controls, a Role dropdown and a Triggers popover with a Done button; a search left empty group headings, a prompt title overflowed a 390px row, the property sheet's switch and number inputs drew at the wrong height on touch, and the editor header's compact tray was shorter than its icon buttons.
- **Files:** `public/scripts/sillybunny-prompt-editor.js` (list header, `createEntityRow` rows, search and the shared "No Results" status page, selection mode, Role and Triggers menus), `public/css/sillybunny-prompt-editor.css`, `public/scripts/sillybunny-editor/chrome.js` (`createTokenReadout`, `setTokens`), `public/css/sillybunny-character-editor.css` (the header tray height).
- **Guards:**
  - The rows are shared entity rows; `sb-prompt-row`, `sb-prompt-row-name`, `sb-prompt-row-subtitle`, `sb-prompt-row-text`, `sb-prompt-grip`, `sb-prompt-token`, `sb-prompt-row-menu` and `data-prompt-id` stay on them, because tests and the agent list read them. The row buttons are plain `button.ds-entity-row__button`, so they take the shared hover and `:active` fills.
  - `.sb-prompt-row { content-visibility: visible }`: `auto` made the restored scroll position land on estimated heights.
  - A disabled row dims by colour (the name takes `--sb-muted-fg`, the avatar `opacity: .6`), not by text opacity, because opacity on the text fails the axe contrast rule.
  - A touch rule `input { min-height: 44px }` inside the property sheet stretched the switch to 46x44; the switch is excluded, and the number inputs are matched as `input[type='number']` at the specificity of the base rule and placed after it, because the base rule outranked the coarse one and left them 36px tall.
  - Role is `openActionMenu` with radio items and Triggers is `openPopover` with check rows and no Done button. Menus, sheets and popovers carry `data-sb-prompt-menu` (`markPromptMenus()`), which `EDIT_SURFACE_SELECTOR` in `sillybunny-preset-autosave.js` reads; `.sb-prompt-popover` is no longer in that selector. The `.sb-prompt-dropdown` rules stay because the agent editor's Category trigger reuses them; the `.sb-prompt-sheet*`, `.sb-prompt-popover` and `.sb-prompt-option*` rules went with the agent editor's own sheet and listbox (task 6.4).
  - A group with no rows is hidden (`syncSectionVisibility`), so a search shows no empty heading; a search that matches nothing shows the shared status page.
  - Utility Prompts, the Agents runtime row and Text Completion rows are not selectable in selection mode (`data-prompt-bulk="false"`).
  - **Heading and search dismissal:** while a sort other than Prompt Order is on, the group heading reads `Main Prompts` (the user's wording); it is the same `h3` and its text follows `sortOrder` in `renderList()`. The prompt search passes `closeEmptyOnBlur: true`, so a press elsewhere closes an empty bar, Sort button included.
  - **Sort:** `.sb-prompt-sort-button` ends the search bar, as World Info's and the persona list's do (`.sb-prompt-search-field.sb-list-search-bar` wraps the entry, now `.sb-prompt-search-entry`, and the button); the list header keeps its three buttons. The sorts are Prompt Order (the default), Title A-Z, Title Z-A, Tokens ↗ and Tokens ↘, saved as `PromptList_SortOrder` in the account storage (`order`, `name-asc`, `name-desc`, `tokens-asc`, `tokens-desc`); a stored value that is none of them reads as `order`. The Chat Completion list is the order the prompts are sent in, so a sort only changes what the list shows: `reorderLocked()` (a search or any sort but `order`) sets the grips' `aria-disabled`, disables the sortable, drops Move Up and Move Down from the row menus and makes Alt+Arrow a no-op. The Prompt Order and Available groups are sorted; the Agents row stays last and the utility prompts keep their fixed order. A row with no count (a marker, an empty prompt, a count not read yet) stays after the counted ones in either token direction. A token sort re-renders once after a pass of `scheduleListTokens` read new counts (`placingCounts` keeps that render from asking for counts again), and `knownCount` of every prompt is part of the list's render key while one is on. The Search toggle and the sort button show `aria-pressed` while a sort other than Prompt Order is on. The Text Completion view (nine fixed templates in three groups of two to four) hides the sort button and ignores a saved sort. The old `.sb-prompt-search-field { margin: 0 8px 8px }` never applied (the bar entry's own margin outranks it), so deleting it changed no layout. The unit tests mock `util/AccountStorage.js`, because the editor imports it. Tests: `tests/unit/sillybunny-prompt-editor.test.js` ("Prompt list sort"), `tests/unit/sillybunny-prompt-editor-templates.test.js`, `tests/prompt-manager-characterization.e2e.js` ("a sort other than the prompt order…").
  - The legacy Editor groups (`#sysprompt_content`, `#InstructSequencesColumn`, `#context_story_string`, the utility textareas, `#sb-openai-prompt-manager`) stay in the DOM, hidden, because `preset-manager.js`, `openai.js` and the Chat Completion tabs read them; only their `.sb-editor-panel` CSS that matched no rendered node was deleted from `sillybunny-tabs.css`.
  - **The old Prompt Manager list is still built, hidden, and its markup follows `staging`:** the editor reads and writes through the `promptManager` object (`handleSavePrompt` with a synthetic `currentTarget`, `handleToggle`, `handleDetach`, `handleSendToAgents`, the edit form's field ids, `render()` and the `sb:prompt-manager-rendered` event), and extensions read the old list: Bunny Preset Tools builds its `li.bpt-section-row` rows inside `#completion_prompt_manager_list` and reads `li.completion_prompt_manager_prompt[data-pm-identifier]`, and ChatCompletionTabs selects `.range-block:has(> #completion_prompt_manager)`. Once the first `synchronize()` succeeds, `.sb-prompt-owned` and `data-sb-prompt-owned` (set in `sillybunny-prompt-editor.js`) make `public/css/sillybunny-prompt-editor.css` (lines 1 to 7) hide `#sb-openai-prompt-manager` and `#completion_prompt_manager_popup` with `display: none !important`. When that sheet fails to load, `stylesReady` stays false and the old list is the working fallback (open, toggle, edit and save a prompt). The row, footer, list header and edit form markup of `PromptManager.js`, its templates and `index.html` is upstream's again, so a `pm-row-*`, `pm-chip-*`, kebab, accordion, trigger-chip or footer-toolbar class has no rule in `public/css/promptmanager.css` and does not exist; give the old list no new styling that the editor does not need, and keep every upstream id, class and `data-pm-*` attribute on it. Reorder-menu names its rows from `.completion_prompt_manager_prompt_name`. Tests: `tests/prompt-manager-role-icon-css.test.js`, `tests/prompt-manager-lifecycle-wiring.test.js`, the Editor page of the settings inventory (`tests/audit/settings-inventory.e2e.js`).
  - `createTokenReadout()` shows the character-design warning link by default and `setTokens()` computes the over-budget flag when the caller omits it (`total` above half the context size, at least 1024), reading the context size from `#main_api` and the max-context counters. The character editor passes the flag itself. World Info, Persona and the Agent editor pass only `total`, so they take the default.
  - In the surface audit, `openPromptEditor` closes a leftover menu before it opens the tab, and `openPromptEditorEntry` clicks the Main row only when the detail is not already open: on a phone the open detail makes the list inert, and a capture that is taken again opens the surface a second time.
  - **Text Completion templates are the same detail as a prompt** (task 6.8), built by the same `renderEditor()`: the shared header (a template has no item menu, so its Edit/Preview toggle stays at every width), the count in the header and the phone summary row (the template's fields joined, as its row counts them), a property bar (Position, Depth, Role of the story string; System same as User, a switch, for the System template) and one `contentField()` per sequence, each with Expand in its label row. The pieces that break if rebuilt:
    - A textarea gets the id `sb-prompt-tc-<field id>`: the legacy `#instruct_*` and `#context_*` fields keep their own ids. It listens through `$(textarea).on('input')`, because the expanded editor writes back with jQuery `.trigger('input')`, which a native listener never hears. The Expand button's accessible description is the field's label, because a template has up to four of them.
    - A name that cannot change (a template, a marker, a utility prompt) is `span.sb-prompt-title.sb-editor-title` with `tabindex="-1"`, not a read-only input: a text input always matches `:focus-visible`, so the title drew a ring or an accent colour whenever a row opened it. Only an editable name is an `<input>` (`#sb-prompt-form_name` is on both).
    - Position and Role are `choiceControl()` menus built from the options of the legacy `#context_story_string_position` and `_role` selects. A template's button is named `Position: <choice>` (`nameWithChoice`), because the visible text must be part of the name (axe `label-content-name-mismatch`); the Role button of a prompt keeps the old name and its baseline records.
    - A disabled template (its instruct or system prompt mode is off) says so in its row's subtitle (`Disabled · …`) and dims; the detail has no footer for it. Every template row has a count slot, `-` while unknown, as a prompt row has.
    - The narrow-sidebar row override (4px padding, 6px gap) skips `.sb-prompt-row--template`, whose rows have no grip, switch or room problem, so they keep the shared 12px.
    - The fields share the pane's height and scroll the body when three lines each do not fit (`.sb-prompt-content--fields`); `tests/sillybunny-backend-settings.e2e.js` asserts the fields' equal height.
    - `editor.prompt.template` has no expand state; the Expand path is covered in `tests/sillybunny-backend-settings.e2e.js`. The expanded editor's textarea has an accessible name now (see the expanded-editor note in the Windows section), so the six `label` records that every editor's `field-expanded` state carried are gone from `tests/fixtures/axe-baseline.json`, and a template expand state would add none.
    - The Text Completion template Preview substitutes macros through `model.preview({ content })` (`manager.preparePrompt` to `substituteParams`), the call the prompt Preview makes, so `{{char}}` and the like resolve in both (`tests/unit/sillybunny-prompt-editor-templates.test.js`, `tests/sillybunny-backend-settings.e2e.js`). The utility prompts' Preview in the same file still shows the raw text.
- **List frame (matches World Info, Persona and Characters):** the prompt page's scroller (`#sb-settings-page [data-sb-panel='editor']:has(.sb-prompt-owned) .sb-shell-panel-scroller`, `sillybunny-prompt-editor.css`) pads like the Characters window's `.scrollableInner` pages: `calc(var(--sb-shell-panel-padding-block) - 4px)` above and `var(--sb-shell-panel-padding-inline)` (32px) at the sides from 1440px, `14px` above and at the sides below. It had the settings page's 24px and 12px, so the card sat 8px lower and 20px further in than World Info's at 1440x900, 10px lower and 2px further out at 390px. The list body pads `8px 0 8px 8px` as World Info's does (`--sb-shell-space-xs`, which keeps the design-rule count), because its right inset is the scrollbar gutter, and the group headings take World Info's `6px 6px 8px` margin and body font through `#sb-settings-page .sb-prompt-editor .sb-prompt-list-body .sb-prompt-section h3`, since the settings page's own `h3` rule (specificity 1,3,0) had made them 12.9px with no side margin. The scrollbar is not the cause: the app draws a 10px scrollbar in every list, and `scrollbar-gutter: stable` reserves it on the prompt, World Info and Persona lists whether or not the rows overflow (the Agents list uses `auto`, so its rows move 10px when it starts to scroll). At 390px the Characters window's scroller reserves its own 10px gutter on the right of the whole page, which the prompt page has not. Test: `tests/prompt-editor-frame.e2e.js`.
- **Triggers has Select All:** six checks make a pick-several list of more than five, so the popover's first row is Select All (`data-sb-select-all`), mixed while some are checked. Checking all six stores all six values, which means the same as none: the summary reads the six names, not "All triggers", until the box is cleared (`tests/prompt-manager-characterization.e2e.js`, "six generation triggers").
- **Tests:** `tests/unit/sillybunny-prompt-editor.test.js`, `tests/unit/sillybunny-prompt-editor-templates.test.js` (Text Completion templates), `tests/unit/sillybunny-editor-chrome-navigation.test.js`, `tests/prompt-manager-characterization.e2e.js`, `tests/sillybunny-preset-autosave.e2e.js`, `tests/prompt-editor-field-contrast.e2e.js`, `tests/in-chat-agents-list.e2e.js`, `tests/sillybunny-backend-settings.e2e.js`; the audit specs for `list.prompt`, `editor.prompt`, `editor.prompt.template`, `menu.prompt-editor.*`, `header.*.prompt`, `panel.sheet.prompt-properties`, `status.prompts.no-results`.
- **Open:** the dirty title of the narrow detail header shows about three characters at 390px, because the shared header gap and padding (6) replaced the narrow override (a decision for the maintainers: restore the override or accept the width); the dirty title has the room of the Edit/Preview toggle since `0e2c3deab` (the toggle moves into the item menu on a phone).
- **Edit/Preview on a phone:** below a 440px detail column the header has no room for the icon toggle beside the name, so the list editors hide it and `mountEditorChrome` puts the switch at the top of the item menu, under the toggle's own name, whenever every control in `controls.modes` is hidden. An item with no menu (a Text Completion template, `.sb-prompt-more[hidden]`) keeps its toggle. The test step `showPreview` (`tests/helpers/surfaces/editor-steps.js`) opens the "… Actions" menu when neither control shows.

### Agents list and editor (list-content editor)

- **Breaks:** the agent editor kept its own header gap and padding and four header buttons that a `ResizeObserver` hid one by one; its property sheet was a bottom sheet of its own with a listbox popover for Category; the list had its own narrow-row padding and a stacked selection bar; and the `#ica--separateRecentChats` switch left the per-chat-type lists seeded after it was switched off again (F-113).
- **Files:** `public/scripts/extensions/in-chat-agents/ui/agent-editor.js`, `agent-editor-properties.js`, `agent-editor.css`, `agent-list.js`, `style.css`, `agent-store.js` (`captureScopedEnableState`, `restoreScopedEnableState`), `index.js` (the switch handler); the persona detail and prompt editor stylesheets for the rules they shared with the agent editor.
- **Guards:**
  - The header holds only Back, name, tokens, Edit/Preview, the item menu, Cancel and Save. Sections, Preview Prompt in Context and Refine… are items of the item menu, titled with the agent's name (`actionMenuTitle`); the item menu is `openActionMenu`, so with the same trigger a second call closes the menu: pressing Agent Actions while Sections is open closes it.
  - Below a 440px detail column both Edit/Preview controls are hidden and `mountEditorChrome` puts the switch first in the item menu; the agent editor builds no item of its own, or the menu would show two.
  - The property sheet is `openDialog({ tier: 'dialog' })` with the classes `sb-prompt-properties-dialog ica-agent-properties-dialog`, and Category is an `openActionMenu` with radio items. A dialog returns focus to what had it when it opened, and a script `click()` does not focus a button, so a spec that opens it that way focuses the summary first. On a phone the menu is a modal sheet: a second click on its trigger cannot reach it, so close it with Escape.
  - Switching `separateRecentChats` on seeds `enabledAgentIdsByChatType` and sets `scopedEnabledAgentIdsInitialized`; switching it off puts both back unless the lists were edited in between. The settings inventory does not see this (it recorded no drift at the base); `tests/in-chat-agents-pages.e2e.js` does.
  - `tests/in-chat-agents-pre-intercept-summary.test.js` lists the `agent-store.js` exports by hand: an export that `index.js` imports must be added to that mock.
  - **List frame (matches the prompt list, World Info and Persona):** the Agents page's scroller (`[data-sb-panel='agents'] .sb-shell-panel-scroller`) shares the prompt page's frame rule in `sillybunny-prompt-editor.css` (`calc(var(--sb-shell-panel-padding-block) - 4px)` above and `var(--sb-shell-panel-padding-inline)` at the sides from 1440px, 14px below). It had the settings page's 24px and 12px, so the card sat 8px lower and 20px further in at 1440x900, and the open agent's header with it. The list body (`.ica-agents-body`) pads `8px 0 8px 8px` with `scrollbar-gutter: stable` as World Info's does; it had `0 12px 16px` and `auto`, so its rows moved from a 12px to a 22px right inset the moment the list scrolled. The group heading (`.ica-agents-stage`) takes the `6px 6px` margin and the `12px 0 4px` padding the prompt and World Info headings carry (their text starts at the same height); the category caption beneath it (`.ica-agents-category`, 16px high) is content the other lists lack, so the first row sits one caption lower. The agents sheets are `style.css` and `ui/agent-editor.css`; the shared frame rule lives in the prompt sheet, which `agent-list.js` loads before the page shows. Test: `tests/prompt-editor-frame.e2e.js` (Agents list and open agent against World Info).
  - The agent list has no narrow-row override: the shared entity row's 12px padding and 10px gap apply at 390px, so the selection title sits at x = 92 there.
- **Tests:** `tests/in-chat-agents-editor.e2e.js`, `in-chat-agents-list.e2e.js`, `in-chat-agents-pages.e2e.js`, `tests/sheet-swipe.e2e.js`, `tests/in-chat-agents-store.test.js`; the audit surfaces `list.agents`, `editor.agent`, `menu.agent*`, `header.*.agent*`, `panel.sheet.agent-properties`.
- **Open:** the `status.agents.no-results` capture times out (130s) at the base as well.

### Audit specs, the surface registry and ratchets

- **Where:** the opt-in config `tests/playwright.audit.config.js` runs the specs in `tests/audit/`, which iterate over the surface registry in `tests/helpers/surfaces/` (one file per family, fixtures in `needs/`). The default, Firefox, WebKit and foundation configs do not run them. The config's global setup seeds every fixture on every server of the run, and `seed-guard.js` refuses a server that the run did not start; port 4444 is always refused.
- **One spec, one boot per surface, viewport and theme:** `tests/audit/surface-audit.e2e.js` runs the smoke, parity and axe checks in the same boots (Libadwaita: each unit is opened once, goes through smoke, parity (first backend's plain open only) and the axe scan, and is closed; Libadwaita Light: the axe units), so a full run boots 1,325 times where three specs booted 2,602. A failure is labelled `smoke:`, `parity:` or `axe:` with the surface and the cell. `SURFACE_CHECKS=smoke,parity,axe` picks the checks (axe only with `SURFACE_AXE_UPDATE=1`); `SURFACE_DENSITY` and `SURFACE_TEXT_SCALE` apply to smoke alone, so with either set only smoke runs. A surface's cells run up to 4 at a time, fewer as the run grows (`min(4, floor(24 / surfaces))`, so 1 from 13 surfaces up and for the whole registry), because each browser context costs about 0.9 GB and the whole registry at 4 cells exhausted the machine's memory (three OOM kills of the agent service on 2026-11-16, before `~/bin/e2e` ran in its own memory-capped slice). The surfaces are counted after `SURFACE_ID` or `SURFACE_FAMILY`; a `--grep` run counts the whole registry and runs one cell at a time. Measured at 4 cells: 5 chat surfaces 71 s (194 s at 1), 10 `chat.roleplay.*` 129 s (417 s), with axe results and census identical. Smoke judges the first open of each unit, so a surface that closes itself is not passed by the axe retry's reopen (`SURFACE_AUDIT_VANISH` proves it).
  - Switching the theme on the Libadwaita page (`applyVariant`) instead of booting again for Light stalled the scan of 36 surfaces and changed the results of 13: a message left in edit mode showed in the Light results of `chat.roleplay.*`. The Light cell boots on its own.
  - A run lasts as long as its longest surface test (`chat.roleplay.flat`, about 700 s of a 724 s run), because a surface's boots run one after the other.
- **A surface must prove that it opened:** `ready` names elements that exist only once the surface or the state is open. A state whose `ready` repeats its entry's passes without having done anything; a review of the chat family found about thirty such states (`9ae1b48a1`). An element at `opacity: 0` counts as visible to Playwright, and a control that is only disabled is still visible.
- **Hover states:** the capture spec moves the pointer out of the page before a screenshot unless the state's id contains `hover`, `focus` or `submenu`. A state that depends on hover has `hover` in its id.
- **Roots in Playwright selector syntax:** a registry root may use `:visible` or `:has-text()`, which `document.querySelectorAll` rejects. Code that queries a root in the page resolves it through a locator first, as `settle()` in `tests/helpers/surfaces/steps.js` does (`c047da3b5`).
- **Design-rule ratchet:** `scripts/audit-design-rules.js` counts restricted CSS patterns per SillyBunny stylesheet, and `tests/design-rule-ratchet.test.js` fails when a count differs from `scripts/design-rule-baseline.json`. A CSS change that moves a count reruns `node scripts/audit-design-rules.js --update` (`--allow-increase` for a rise) in the same commit (`2da6312ed`).
  - `spacing-off-scale` counts each px literal of padding, margin and gap that is not 0 and not on the spacing scale of `DESIGN.md` (3, 6, 9, 12, 18, 24, 36px); `var()` fallbacks count apart. A rebuilt surface lowers it; the baseline started at 1,489 in the SillyBunny sheets.
- **Parity allow-list:** the parity check of `tests/audit/surface-audit.e2e.js` compares parts of each surface with the family's canonical values (`tests/helpers/parity/`). A difference must be in `tests/fixtures/parity-allowlist.json`, and an entry whose difference no longer occurs fails the run, so a fix removes its entries and lowers the count with `node scripts/update-parity-allowlist.js --lower` (`d1e41b749`).
  - **An `own` alternative that matches by class substring or by tag measures what it should not:** `[class*="title" i]` read `__subtitle` elements as titles (header and status families), and the plain `button` of the menu `item` read a sheet's close button, a popover's response buttons and its button rows as menu items. The header and status `own` title selectors now exclude `subtitle`, and a menu `own` item is `a`, `li`, a `[role^="menuitem"]`, a `[role="option"]` or a `button` inside a `[role="menu"]` or `[role="listbox"]`. 251 entries went (header 133 to 93, menu 565 to 362, status 70 to 62), each traced to the element it measured; a changed title or item still fails (`SB_PARITY_INJECT_CSS`). Header title inputs stay measured: the editors' title is the input. Left as they were: the `titles` wrapper and the dialog's own `h2.sb-dialog__title` that `status.home.about-unknown` measures as status titles.
  - **The same element in two alternatives counts as a markup outlier:** `[class*="name" i]` in the list family's `OWN_TITLE` also matched an entity row's title (`ch_name`), its text wrapper (`group_member_name`) and the date and size beside a swipe's file name (`select_chat_block_filename_item`), so rows that are entity rows reported `own` markup. The rule now excludes what is inside `.ds-entity-row` and those two classes (`tests/helpers/parity/list.js`); the editor's `entry` part leaves out the chip entry's typing input and the header's editable title for the same reason (`tests/helpers/parity/editor.js`).
  - **A stacked entry row is an `own` field, a floating one is the row:** `createEntryRow` without `floating: true` leaves a boxed `<input>` that the editor family measures at 38px with 6px padding; `floating: true` makes the row the field. The Persona Title and the agent text fields moved to it, and the Persona stylesheet's boxed-input rules now skip `.ds-row-entry--floating`. The Quick Reply editor's Label, Title and Automation ID rows still use the stacked form: with `floating: true` their field collapsed under the upstream `.qr--labels` rules of `quick-reply/style.css`.
  - **A status page built at startup costs a resource:** `createStatusPage` loads `sillybunny-editor-shell.css` the first time it runs, and `frontend-performance.e2e.js` caps the startup resources (`STARTUP_RESOURCE_LIMIT`). The Backgrounds Chat tab hint (`backgrounds.js`) is built when it first scrolls into view for that reason.
  - **`LIBADWAITA_AUDIT_ALLOWLIST` entries are narrowed to the failing element:** the page-level exemptions of the tab roots and `#user-settings-block` hid real, specific failures (the spin-row suffix at 390px, the subpage row's icon inset, the background controls' last border). With an empty list the settings family fails only at those elements, so each entry names one (`tests/helpers/libadwaita-audit.js`). Entries for pages that no settings surface renders (the Prompt Manager, the text-completion context group, the instruct textareas) stay, unmeasured.
- **Captures and the pixel comparison:** `tests/audit/surface-capture.e2e.js` saves a capture of every surface, state and scrolled page, and compares it with a baseline that lives outside the repository (`SB_VISUAL_BASELINE_DIR`). What made two captures of the same tree differ, and what guards against each:
  - A mask covers its element's box also when another surface lies on top, so a mask on the Home art blanked the centre of every other surface. A mask is painted only where its element is on show.
  - The page behind a dialog, a menu or a sheet differs in scroll position between runs. An overlay is compared only inside the box of its root; the whole window is still saved.
  - Motion that script drives (the Home figures) is held still for the length of a shot; Playwright's `animations: 'disabled'` covers CSS and Web Animations only.
  - A chat transcript is not paged: the app measures a message's height when the row comes into view, so page offsets differ (`pagesFor()` in `tests/helpers/capture-plan.js`).
  - The comparison threshold of 0.02 hides a grey difference of up to 5 levels (9 red, 7 green, 15 blue), and up to three pixels above it pass (`NOISE_PIXELS`). Both are listed in the summary under "Same under the threshold, not identical", so that a low-contrast change is seen by a person.
  - Recording into a baseline directory that holds captures is refused unless `SB_VISUAL_RECORD=replace`, because a leftover record flag would replace the baseline with the present tree and every later comparison would read "same".
- **A settings subsection whose level depended on timing:** `flattenNestedSettingsDrawers()` (`public/scripts/sillybunny-settings-content.js`) made a drawer a subsection only when a drawer was around it in that run. A drawer built inside a section that was already flat, or flattened before its page builder moved it into a boxed row, became a top-level section, so the same page had one of two layouts per load (Chat Completion Presets and Sampling). A flat section and a boxed group or row now count as enclosing, and a section that was moved into a box loses the top-level class on the next run (`5445bb874`, `58f53e983`). Tests: `tests/unit/sillybunny-settings-content.test.js`. The capture comparison found it: the page was a few pixels taller in some runs.
- **What an audit boot fixes:** the server's git state and its `config.yaml` differ between two checkouts and between two moments of one, and Data & Security shows both, so `bootSurfaceApp()` answers those reads with fixed data (`tests/helpers/surfaces/server-admin-fixed.js`, `331471f87`, `bd78f10b9`). A fixture that must hold against a later preset application edits the presets of the settings answer through its `answer` hook, as `needs/logit-bias.js` does: applying a Chat Completion preset sets the logit bias selection from the preset.
- **Surfaces that still differ between two captures now and then:** Visual Novel mode (the expressions extension rebuilds its holders on a poll), the persona menu with no personas at 390x844 (it was not open at one capture in three runs; cause not found), and a code block whose highlighting lands after the capture. The summary of a comparison names them.
  - **Tag chips of a character row (F-118):** they are fitted once per container width (`fitTagStrips()` in `public/scripts/sillybunny-editor/entity-row.js`), so two rows of the character list showed one chip more or less between loads at 390x844. The cause was assumed to be the web font, which changes chip widths and not the strip's. `limitTagChips()` now keeps the observed strips and measures them again, once per frame, when `document.fonts` fires `loadingdone` (`refitStripsAfterFontLoad()`; `tests/unit/sillybunny-entity-row.test.js` "measures every observed strip again…"). The mechanism is tested; that it removes the run-to-run difference is not shown by a repeated capture.
- **Accessibility baseline:** the axe check of `tests/audit/surface-audit.e2e.js` scans every surface with axe-core inside the surface's root and holds the violations of the WCAG A and AA rules in `tests/fixtures/axe-baseline.json`. The section of a violation follows the element, not the surface: on a surface of owner B or with `upstreamMarkup`, an element is in the `sillybunny` section only when its own id or class starts with `sb-`, `ds-` or `sillybunny`. By surface alone, 97 SillyBunny elements sat in the `upstream` section. Records are compared cell by cell (theme, viewport, backend), so a fix in one theme is a removal and not a new record. `best-practice` rules are reported and not gated, because their landmark and heading rules judge the whole document. `@axe-core/playwright` is pinned to an exact version, because a change of axe's rules moves the baseline (`aba00ed7b`).
  - **A caption is dimmed once:** upstream's `small { opacity: 0.7 }` (`public/style.css`) dimmed a SillyBunny caption a second time over `--sb-muted-fg`, which took the dark tone to about 38% of the body colour, under 4.5:1. The captions of settings sections, subpages and sampler tools keep full opacity in `public/css/sillybunny-theme.css`, so the token is the only dimming (`9dcdbbd7d`). An element that takes its dim strength from `--sb-muted-fg` must not also sit in an upstream rule that sets `opacity`; axe measures the product.
- **Copy baseline:** the `copy` check of `tests/audit/surface-audit.e2e.js` judges the Capitalisation Rule on the text that the registry's surfaces render, each string in the form of its rendered role (`tests/helpers/copy-collect.js` decides the role in the page; `tests/helpers/capitalisation.js` is the rule, shared with `sillybunny-label-capitalisation.test.js`; `tests/helpers/copy-judge.js` adds the placeholders and the sentence-length verdict). It reads both viewports of the Libadwaita boot and judges the union per surface or state. Records are `{surface, state?, role, text, expected, target}` in `tests/fixtures/copy-baseline.json`, in two sections as the axe baseline: `upstream` for a string that is verbatim in the upstream base commit, `sillybunny` for the rest, which an update never adds to without `SURFACE_COPY_ALLOW_SILLYBUNNY=1`. A run fails on a violation in neither section and on a record that no longer occurs.
  - **Label or data:** a rendered text counts only when a file of `public/` holds it as a string (`scripts/copy-strings.js`: literals, template text with `${}` holes, HTML text, attributes, JSON; a text that fills a template is judged as the template). Anything else is data (names, files, dates, model ids). Not judged either: text that only quick-image-gen, third-party extensions, extension libraries or the bundled agent templates hold, names in `default/content/index.json`, identifiers and paths (`eye-slash`, `/user/files/x`), and the five random titles of the empty Characters page. The upstream and tree sets are cached in the temp directory, keyed by file sizes and times and a `CACHE_VERSION` that the extraction raises.
  - **What it misses:** text built from parts that no file holds whole, bold text that is not in a heading element or an element with `title` in its class, a rendered role that the page decides some other way, state the registry does not open, and text of a surface that the smoke check cannot open (`menu.agents.add-menu`, `status.import.loading` at the time). The recorded counts and the method are in the report of task 12.5b.
- **Settings inventory:** `tests/audit/settings-inventory.e2e.js` checks every control of every settings page against `tests/fixtures/settings-inventory.json`, and for value controls that a change through the UI reaches the same saved key. A forced save writes the whole in-memory state, so the check watches for the control's own save and does not flush; a pending debounced save of the previous control would otherwise be taken for the next one's (`d6330ceb9`). A control may move to another group without a failure; any other intended difference needs an entry in `ACCEPTED_DIFFERENCES` of `tests/helpers/settings-inventory-rules.js`.
  - **Wrapping controls in a field renames them:** `identityOf()` in `tests/helpers/settings-inventory-collect.js` names a control that has no id, `name` or `data-*` hook after the `data-sb-editor-field` of the nearest row (`field:<id>:<kind>`), and only then after its accessible name. The sampler cards turned into expander rows that are fields (`sampler-<id>`), so the five help links of Adaptive-P, XTC and DRY changed identity and the inventory failed until `tests/fixtures/settings-inventory.json` recorded them again (`ab6a786a0`).
  - **A row's field hook outranks its own `data-*` hook:** `hooksOf()` sorts a control's data attributes by name and takes the first, and `data-sb-editor-field` sorts before `data-sb-subpage-row`. The Extensions list rows moved onto `createRow()`, which writes the field, so the rows' identity went from `data:data-sb-subpage-row=<container>` to `data:data-sb-editor-field=subpage@as:<title>`, a key that changes when a title does. The stack now passes the container id as the field (`setRows` in `public/scripts/sillybunny-settings-subpage.js`), and the fixture records `data:data-sb-editor-field=<container>`. A field name that several rows share is suffixed with the folded name.
  - **A dependent set in a closed expander was recorded disabled:** `enableDependentSets()` skips a set that is not shown and a switch that is not visible, so Dynamic Temperature's range rows (a `data-sb-enabled-by` set inside a closed sampler expander) were recorded `disabled`, without `keys` or `selfSaves`, and no round trip ran on them. `collectSection()` now enables the sets again once `expandAll()` has opened the expanders, and its caller gives those switches back (`tests/helpers/settings-inventory-steps.js`, `tests/helpers/settings-inventory-run.js`). A control that must stay usable inside a dependent set that is off (the sampler help toggle, which the harness presses to open) carries `data-sb-keeps-sensitive`, and `setRowInsensitive()` skips it.
- **An open step returns when the page is built (F-304):** Appearance, Interface, Messages (all built by `sillybunny-appearance.js`), Formatting and Presets (`sillybunny-backend-pages.js`), Sampling, Data & Security and Logs build after their module loads, and a read before then sees the raw upstream rows or a loading status. `openSettingsTab()` (`tests/helpers/surfaces/steps.js`), which every settings surface's `open` goes through, calls `waitForPageBuilt()`: no `aria-busy` on `[data-sb-panel="<tab>"]` or on a direct child of its scroller. Appearance, Interface and Messages (`initializeSettingsPanel`) and Formatting and Presets (`ensureBackendPages`) had no mark; `markPanelsBusyUntilBuilt()` (`public/scripts/sillybunny-tabs.js`) sets it on their panels until the build settles. CPU load alone (128 busy loops, load about 150, the whole inventory at `-s 3 -w 8`) did not reproduce the failure on the old code; slowing the module imports does. Test: `tests/audit/settings-open-wait.e2e.js` delays the five modules by 6 s and reads each page the moment its open step returns (7 of 8 fail on the old code; Sampling already waited through the logit bias step).
- **The design documents state patterns, and a test holds them to it:** `DESIGN.md` and `DESIGN.json` describe patterns, roles and conditions, never one feature. `tests/unit/design-documents-generality.test.js` fails when either names a feature, page, window or bundled extension from its list; add a name to the list when a feature gets one. What is true of one feature lives in `docs/feature-design-rules.md` (`6eb9df89e`, `87f1dde65`).
  - A deviation has one form: what the reference does; what SillyBunny does, because of a reason; when to review it. A deviation without a reason is not written by an agent: the user gives the reason, or the app follows the reference.
  - The documents may state a rule that the code does not meet yet, with a clause "Existing … move to this rule as each surface is rebuilt". Delete the clause in the commit that makes the rule true.
- **The parity rules follow the design documents:** a canonical value in `tests/helpers/parity/` that cites `DESIGN.md` changes when the documents do. Record the allow-list again afterwards (`SB_PARITY_RECORD=1 SB_CENSUS_DIR=<dir> SURFACE_CHECKS=parity` with `audit/surface-audit.e2e.js` under the audit config, then `node scripts/update-parity-allowlist.js --census <dir> --write --allow-increase`), and state the rise in the commit (`f56c6d920`: 1,441 to 1,578 temporary entries for the decided values).
  - **A family's canonical value is not a rule for every member:** task 14.6e set the Extensions manager's rows to the list family's `52px` and `10px` gap to clear `list.extensions-manager`, and `tests/sillybunny-backend-settings.e2e.js` ("Agents globals and Connections switches use the shared row semantics", `expectSwitchGeometry`) failed: those rows are switch rows in a dialog (`50px`, `6px` gap), and `DESIGN.md` gives the `52px` minimum to rows in list sidebars. The size and gap rules are removed (merge of 14.6d, 2026-11-18) and the difference is a `registered` entry in `tests/fixtures/parity-allowlist.json` that cites the section. Before clearing a parity entry by changing a size, find the tests that hold the element's own pattern.
- **The reference of the design documents:** both record a libadwaita release and a commit of the GNOME HIG, and `tests/unit/design-documents-reference.test.js` fails when they disagree or lose either. `node scripts/check-design-reference.js` asks gitlab.gnome.org whether the reference has moved; it needs the network and is never run by the test suite (`87f1dde65`). A statement may also name a GNOME Shell release (`GNOME Shell 51.1`), which the script compares with the latest stable `major.point` tag of `GNOME/gnome-shell` (the `.alpha`, `.beta` and `.rc` snapshots are skipped); with no Shell release recorded it does not compare the Shell and does not call the statement unreadable (`tests/design-reference.test.js`, `90334d432`). The statement of DESIGN.md and DESIGN.json names GNOME Shell 51.1 (`343554250`), the release whose banner files (`messageTray.js`, `messageList.js`, `_notifications.scss`, `_message-list.scss`) the in-app notification follows. Reading them at tag 51.1 found one value that differed, and `343554250` took Shell's: buttons and rows of a notification are 8px apart (4px of margin on each side of a button, none at the ends of the row; the `spacing: 6px` of `.message-action-bin` has no effect on an `St.Bin`) where DESIGN.md and the row had 6px. A release is read when the statement ends a sentence too (`GNOME Shell 51.1.`): the pattern for the version was `(\d+\.\d+)(?![\d.])`, which refused a point that follows the release, and the script then took the Shell as not recorded; it is `(?!\.?\d)` (`tests/design-reference.test.js`). The Design Reference workflow (`.github/workflows/design-reference.yml`) runs it when `staging` moves to a new `X.Y.0-dev` version, and keeps one open `design-reference` issue while the reference has moved.
- **A settings row gets its metrics from tokens, and a literal in a more specific rule hides them:** `.ds-row` (`public/css/sillybunny-tabs.css`) reads `--sb-density-row-min-height`, `--sb-density-row-padding-*`, `--sb-density-row-gap` and `--sb-density-row-text-gap`, and the page reads `--sb-settings-group-gap`, `--sb-settings-heading-gap` and `--sb-settings-inset*` (`public/css/sillybunny-theme.css`). Removing the old `#sb-settings-page .ds-row-switch` rule showed the older rules under it: `.checkbox_label` and `.range-block` rules scoped by `#openai_api`, the `[data-tg-samplers]` card column, `.sb-topbar-label-option { min-height: auto }` at 768px and below, and the `(pointer: coarse)` list that gives `.ds-row` in Sampling a literal 44px. Each now excludes `.ds-row` or reads the token. The Sampling rebuild later deleted the `[data-tg-samplers]` card column and the Sampling 44px list, so its rows read the tokens alone. A new row rule is checked by `expectSwitchGeometry` (`tests/sillybunny-backend-settings.e2e.js`, `tests/sillybunny-settings-consistency.e2e.js`), `tests/sillybunny-connections-layout.e2e.js` (rows compared with the shared row) and the `row` part of `tests/helpers/parity/settings.js`.
  - **A row has no second line of controls:** the wire control of a sampler (Auto, Send, Omit) was once a full-width line inside its slider row, which made the row taller and put the slider 22px above its midpoint; the row-geometry check of the parity spec failed for four Chat Completion samplers. It is a toggle row of its own after the slider row (`addWireRow()` in `public/scripts/sillybunny-sampling.js`), so the check needs no exception. Run the parity spec of every surface a change touches, not only the one the task names.
- **A dialog row takes the same metrics as a settings row, and a fallback rule at higher specificity hides them:** the dialog family's `row` part (`tests/helpers/parity/dialog.js`, `ROW_METRICS`, shared with the settings family) holds the 50px minimum and the 6px gap of every `.ds-row` in a dialog, which found rows at 44px with a 16px inset (`.sb-dialog .ds-row.checkbox_label`), a 12px gap (`dialog.popup.sb-dialog .checkbox_label`, the fallback for unconverted labels, now `:not(.ds-row)`), an 8px gap on stacked rows, a 44px Run Test button row (`regex/debugger.css`) and 10px block padding on property rows (`.ds-row-property`, which the settings groups had overridden). The dialog family holds at most 20 properties (`tests/unit/parity-allowlist.test.js`), so the part measures the height and the gap only; the padding is the settings family's. The art dialogs keep 44px, 16px and 12px through `.sb-dialog:has(.sbArtFigure)`, because their height decides where the art stands on the pixel grid (`tests/art-pixel-evenness.e2e.js`). Tests: `tests/audit/surface-audit.e2e.js` (parity, `SURFACE_FAMILY=dialog`).
- **A surface with no status page is not measured as one:** `status.home.about-unknown` is a property row of the About window and `status.sampling.unavailable` is an inert row of a boxed list with a badge, so the status census read the dialog frame's title wrapper, its Close button, the About rows and the badge icon as status parts. Both are in the family's `skip` (`tests/helpers/parity/status.js`) with the reason, and their 16 entries are gone. A surface whose open step shows no `.sb-status-page`, heading or action is a candidate for the same.
  - **No width breakpoint:** `.ds-row` wraps (`flex-wrap`), the text takes `flex: 1 1 var(--sb-row-title-min)` and a control keeps its own basis, so a control drops under its title only when both do not fit, and the threshold follows the text size. A row whose children cannot wrap sets `flex-wrap: nowrap`: the floating entry row, `.ds-row--stacked`, and the extension manager row (`.extension_block`, whose text block is `display: contents` over `nowrap` children; with wrapping on, the switch dropped to a second line).
  - **A row title breaks only at a word boundary (F-306):** `.ds-row-text` had `min-width: 0` and `overflow-wrap: anywhere`. With `anywhere`, the title's minimum width is one letter, so where the title shares its text box with a button (the sampler help toggle, 44px on a phone) the title shrank by a few pixels and "Temperature" broke as "Temperatu/re" at 390 and 360px. The title column now keeps its automatic minimum (its longest word and the button), `max-width: 100%` clamps that to the row, and `overflow-wrap: break-word` breaks a word only when it cannot fit on a line of its own; the row wraps its control under the title first. Not changed: the entry rows (`.ds-row-entry__caption`) and the legacy flex-container labels (`min-width: min(var(--sb-row-title-min), 100%)`), which break a word wider than their own 7em box. Test: `tests/sillybunny-row-title-wrap.e2e.js` (a touch phone at 390, 360 and 320px, Chat Completion and Aphrodite, every explanation open; 4 of 9 fail on the old sheet).
  - **A dialog with art keeps its rows:** the Welcome page stands on the device pixel grid only at the heights its rows gave it, and rows of 50px with a 57.3px subtitle row made `tests/art-pixel-evenness.e2e.js` fail at scale 3. `.sb-dialog:has(.sbArtFigure)` in `sillybunny-theme.css` resets the row tokens to 44px, 16px and 12px and restores the 52px subtitle row.
  - **Editor sheets that pad their own fields read the inset token:** `.ds-field` and the former Created by and Character Version pair (`.ds-field-pair > .ds-field`, gone since the two are entry rows) in `public/css/sillybunny-character-editor.css` had 16px literals, so Created by stood 4px right of the notes field once the non-row children of a settings group took 12px (`tests/character-editor-rendering.e2e.js`).
  - **Probe with the CSSOM, not by reading rules:** `getComputedStyle` says what a row got, and a walk over `document.styleSheets` for rules that match the element and set `min-height`, `padding` or `gap` says who set it. Run on an audit surface, it found each of the above in one pass.
  - **A rewritten root block keeps every token it held:** `4e03c17e8` rewrote the `:root` density block of `sillybunny-theme.css` and dropped `--sb-icon-control-radius`. The token stayed declared only on `.sb-header-icon-button` (`sillybunny-editor-shell.css`), so the icon-only `.menu_button` controls, `.editor_maximize`, the inline drawer icons and the editor icon buttons that read it without a fallback drew square corners, while the prompt manager's copy with a `10px` fallback stayed round. The token is declared at the root again, and `tests/density-tokens-css.test.js` ("shared tokens") fails when a token that two or more shared sheets read without a fallback has no root declaration. To find what an undeclared token changes, compare `getComputedStyle` of every element before and after `:root { --name: initial; }`.
- **A surface is measured as the pattern it is (`tests/helpers/parity/`):** `list.backgrounds` is a thumbnail grid (`DESIGN.md` Grid Views), so it is not in `READY_ROWS`, and `status.home.offline` is a link button on each card, so the status rules skip it. `list.extensions-manager` keeps upstream's switch rows and their delegated `btn_*` handlers, so its markup differences are `registered` entries (a markup entry may be registered, with `viewport: "*"`). The swipe picker's rows are the chat-file template block with the shared entity row classes (`public/scripts/swipe-picker.js` keeps the ids, `file_name` and `data-swipe-id`, its select button and its hidden upstream controls), and the picker loads `sillybunny-entity-list.css` before it draws them. A hairline between its rows is `.select_chat_block_wrapper + .select_chat_block_wrapper`, because each row sits in a wrapper of its own and `:last-child` is true for all of them.
- **The archive writes its filter to the server (F-302):** typing in the archive search (`list.archive` search-results types `audit-archive-2`) makes it write `_sbca_organization.json` (`lastView`, debounced 500 ms) through `/api/files/upload`, which `protectMaintainerServer()` does not stub. Any later archive boot on that server loaded it ("1 matching chats for…", "1 of 9 indexed chat files"). `bootSurfaceApp()` calls `keepArchiveOrganizationOffServer()` (`tests/helpers/surfaces/boot.js`): the upload is answered without writing and the file reads as 404. `tests/audit/boot-isolation.e2e.js` fails without it.
- **Playwright's `click()` scrolls the clipped page behind a dialog:** the `install-extension` open step clicked `#third_party_extension_button`, and the scroll-into-view moved the clipped `div.sb-subpage` (scrollLeft 12) and the scroller (scrollTop 24), so the Extensions page showed shifted sideways behind the dialog in 3 of 4 runs alone. The step uses `openExtensionsList()` and a DOM click (`dialog-form.js`). A comparison that covers only the dialog box lists such a shift as "outside the compared box". Other bare `openTab('right', …)` opens (`dialog-form.js`, `dialog-window.js`, `dialog-alert.js`, `list-steps.js`, `menu.js`, `menu-steps.js`, `editor.js`) were not changed.
- **A capture cell and a retake start from a loaded page:** with `CAPTURE_BOOT=reuse` the cells of one surface and viewport share a page, and a retake ran on the page of the first pass. The state that a cell left (a typed search, a filter, selection mode, an open wand menu) made the next open step wait for what never showed: a toggle-style open (`openLegacyToggle()` on `#extensionsMenuButton`) closed the open menu and `settleFade()` waited for the test timeout (`list.persona` search-results, `list.worldinfo-entries` and `status.agents.no-results` in Libadwaita Light, `menu.composer.wand` in a retake). `rebootSurfaceApp()` (`boot.js`) reloads the page, accepts a `beforeunload` prompt (an editor with a draft asks, and a dialog nobody answers cancels the reload), and applies the variant; `loadAgain()` in `tests/audit/surface-capture.e2e.js` uses it for each cell after the first and before each retake, and adds `CAPTURE_INJECT_CSS` again. A retake also runs the surface's `close` step after a dry pass. Baselines of non-first variant cells that were recorded on a reused page can hold the state of the cell before (the as-found Libadwaita Light `list.persona` was captured in selection mode) and are recorded again.
- **A picker that is rebuilt is hidden for a frame:** `refreshOpenPersonaPicker()` (`public/scripts/sillybunny-tabs.js`) removed the open persona picker and built a new one on a persona or settings event, hidden at 0,0 until the next frame. `prepareForCapture()` (`tests/helpers/capture-page.js`) checked the root right after it blurred the page, found it hidden and opened the surface again, and the open step's click on the bubble closed the picker that was still there (`#sb-persona-picker: none`, 1 to 5 % of captures under load). The new picker takes the old one's position (`place`) and stays shown; `tests/persona-picker-dismiss.e2e.js` reads it on six frames after `PERSONA_UPDATED`. `openPersonaMenu()` clicks the bubble only when no picker exists. Refreshing still drops the focus that was inside the picker.
