A stub does nothing, returns the type that Showdown returns, and logs one warning for each method name for the page, because `reloadMarkdownProcessor()` builds a new converter whenever "Non-markdown strings" changes. `tests/markdown-engine-converter-api.test.js` lists the methods and fails when one is missing from either converter.
- **A browser test that holds the chat read does not check the order of the save and the reload:** the change handler stores the engine, saves, then awaits `reloadCurrentChat()`. With the handler's own save left out, a save that carried the new engine still arrived while the chat read was held, so another path saves settings during a reload, and a test of that kind passes for either order. `tests/markdown-engine-wiring.test.js` checks the order in the source.
- **Tests:** `tests/markdown-engine.test.js`, `tests/markdown-engine-parity.test.js`, `tests/markdown-engine-wiring.test.js`, `tests/markdown-engine-converter-api.test.js`, `tests/unit/sillybunny-markdown-engine-row.test.js`; `tests/markdown-engine.e2e.js` (Customize > Messages at 1440x900 and 390x844, with Auto-fix Markdown off and on, a saved Showdown choice, an unknown choice and a reload).

### Label capitalisation and writing style (cross-cutting)

- **The rule is enforced from the source, in three files:** `tests/helpers/capitalisation.js` holds the rule (`checkLabel(role, text)`; the register script of the copy sweep implements the same rule, so change both together), `tests/helpers/label-reader.js` reads role-tagged labels with acorn and parse5, and `tests/unit/sillybunny-label-capitalisation.test.js` fails with the file, line, role, string and the form it should have. Exemptions are in `tests/fixtures/label-capitalisation-allowlist.json`, one reason each; the test fails on an entry that no longer matches.
- **A helper that takes its label by position is read only when it is listed in `POSITIONAL_HELPERS`:** the register script reads object keys and calls it knows by name, so the eight `createServerButtonRow('…', …)` labels and the six thumbnail rows of `sillybunny-server-tools.js` were not rows until a reader looked at the calls. A new helper of this shape needs an entry, keyed by the file that defines it.
- **A check box that the settings pass draws as a switch row has a row title, so it takes header capitalisation, though its markup says check label:** the settings inventory (`tests/fixtures/settings-inventory.json`) records the rendered switch names, and the test places each name in the source. A name that only unmarked lines of an upstream-origin file hold is upstream wording. The inventory is the data, so a markup edit alone is not seen until the inventory is recorded again (`settings-inventory.e2e.js` compares both).
- **An upstream key is never renamed; SillyBunny code points at a header-case key beside it:** where SillyBunny code (not an upstream line it left alone) writes a string whose key a locale translates, `public/locales/*.json` gets the header-case key with the same translated value in each locale that holds the old key (`Move up` and `Move Up`, `Search...` and `Search…`), and the code uses the new key. The test still skips every string that is a locale key, so an upstream line keeps its sentence-case key. A key that no locale holds changes together with its visible text (`Auto-Close Setting Drawers`). Insert the new line textually next to the old one: ru-ru.json repeats some old keys, so a script that adds per match writes the new key twice, and `locale-validators.test.js` fails on the repeat.
- **A toast message is body text (sentence capitalisation) and a toast title is a title:** the prefix of the Guided Swipe toasts is read back by `startsWith` in `guidedSwipe.js`, so the prefix (`Guided Swipe error:`) is one constant spelling across the seven toasts and the code that reads it.
- **An upstream line that SillyBunny touched for another reason keeps its upstream heading:** `Popup.show.confirm('Replace existing Quick Reply Set', …)` carries a `SillyBunny:` marker for its verb response, so the test reads the heading as SillyBunny-written; such headings are in the allowlist with that reason.
- **Row subtitles, captions and descriptions of one sentence end without a period (`DESIGN.md`, HIG Writing Style):** the 110 single-sentence `<small class="ds-row-subtitle">` strings of `index.html` and the `subtitle:`, `description:` and `caption:` values of SillyBunny scripts lost it; text of two sentences keeps its periods, and so does a string that is verbatim in the upstream base (a key that locales translate) and every toast message. The test reads header-role labels only, so a period on a description is not caught by it; `settings-copy` helpers and tests that assert subtitle text (`tests/helpers/copy-settings.js`) move with the text.
  - **The period rule is a source check for captions and row subtitles, and the rendered check leaves it out:** `checkLabel()` (`tests/helpers/capitalisation.js`) fails a one-sentence `row subtitle` or `caption` that ends in a period (`PERIOD_ROLES`; several sentences keep theirs), and `label-reader.js` reads `description` as a `caption` when a status page, row or group factory takes it or the object also holds a `title` (or an `id` and a `label`), reads the description argument of `createBackendGroupHeader`, `createGroup` and `createPromptingGroup` (a `POSITIONAL_HELPERS` entry may be a list), and reads a three-element `[id, title, description]` array in the files of `ROW_TABLE_FILES`. `tests/helpers/copy-judge.js` judges a rendered one-sentence caption or row subtitle for its period only when `copy-origin.js` classes the string as `sillybunny` (`judgeRendered(role, text, { origin })`); an `upstream` string, or one of unknown origin, keeps its wording and its period, because the rule is for text SillyBunny wrote. The first run with the check on held 181 SillyBunny strings, 44 distinct (settings and character editor subtitles from `index.html` titles and `ds-field-subtitle` markup, the bundled-extras tile descriptions, the settings subpage descriptors); they lost their period, and the `data-i18n` keys that carry them moved with the text. A sentence tooltip whose text became a subtitle is the same record without its period in `copy-baseline.json` (`copy-baseline.test.js` reads a record that only lost a final period as already held). A text with a bullet after a period (`Auto: ... • Send: ...`) is several sentences. A status note that joins sentences at run time (`sillybunny-server-tools.js`) joins period-free parts, because the template of its first part would otherwise be judged as a one-sentence caption: `stringLookup()` used to let a template's last hole swallow any text that ends in the template's closing chunk, so SillyBunny-edited strings (`Omit World Info and Author's Note ... Main API.`, the three extension-manager tooltips) counted as `upstream`; a hole now stops at the end of a sentence (`crossesSentence()`), and those seven records (the tooltips on two surfaces and the Summarize tooltip) moved from the `upstream` to the `sillybunny` section of `copy-baseline.json`. Tests: `tests/unit/copy-origin.test.js` ("stringLookup across sentences"). Tests: `tests/unit/sillybunny-label-capitalisation.test.js` (the period rule and the reader).
- **A no-result status page carries one guidance line, and its title is `No Results Found`:** every status page with the `no-results` state (character and group lists, Persona, World Info and its settings, Agents, Tags, Folders, Chat History, Data Bank, backgrounds, prompts, the assistant picker, the shared picker, Help, Shortcuts, the group member lists) has the title `No Results Found` and the description `Try a different search` (libadwaita's wording, no final period). The page does not default it, because `status-page.js` imports no translation module and many unit tests load it bare, so each caller passes the string through `t`. The settings search, the Home recent-chat filter (`No Matching Chats`), the Conversation timeline (`No Matching Messages`) and the Chats Archive keep their own wording. The shared picker takes its no-results title from the caller's `emptyText` (about forty callers pass `No Results`), so its title is still `No Results`; only its description is added.
- **The character editor's menu leaves out what another place offers:** `_buildActionSections()` in `public/scripts/sillybunny-character-editor.js` skips Convert to Persona (the character list's row menu and selection mode carry it) and, while editing a card that has no linked book, Link to World Info (the Related group shows that row). Both stay in the transport select, which the list and the Related row dispatch. `tests/character-editor-rendering.e2e.js` ("holds twelve items at most") counts the items at 1440 and 390 without the toolbar buttons that fold into the menu.
- **A `SillyBunny:` marker above a statement makes every label in that statement SillyBunny-written:** put the marker at the end of the line of the changed string, after the label the test reads, when the statement also holds upstream labels (`emptyText: t`No Results`, // SillyBunny: …` in `tags.js` after `label: t`Select tag to merge into``).
- **Loading pages name what loads; only the boot splash's flavour lines are in character:** the flavour lines of `sillybunny-splash.js` are in character on purpose (a Touhou Project reference) and are not interface copy. A loading status page has a title that names what loads, in header capitalisation and without an ellipsis (`Loading Characters`, `Loading Settings`, `Loading Chats`, `Loading Chat`, `Importing Content`, and `Settings Not Loaded` for its error page), and `tests/helpers/copy-judge.js` no longer skips any loading title. A deferred Characters panel takes its title from `getDeferredPanelLoadingTitle(panelId)` in `sillybunny-tabs.js`, one whole string per panel, so a translation keeps its word order. The archive's `Loading Chat` and `Loading Chats` keys sit beside the old `…` keys in the locales that held them, and the added values still end in `…` until a translator changes them.
- **A toast title is an informal heading in sentence capitalisation (HIG Toasts), and the helper reads it strictly:** `'toast title'` is in `SENTENCE_ROLES` of `tests/helpers/capitalisation.js`, and `checkLabel` fails any word after the first that is not a protected name, an acronym or an inner capital (`Delete Failed` is wrong, `Delete failed` right; `Quick Reply init failed`, `API error` and `SillyBunny palette` keep their names). A title as long as a sentence still skips as `sentence-length`. Upstream titles keep their wording and sit in files the reader skips or in locales; the 127 SillyBunny-written titles that were in header case now read in sentence case, and `zh-cn.json` holds `Default persona deleted` and `Persona deleted` beside the upstream header-case keys. The reader does not see a title that passes through a variable (`showRequestError({ title })`, the connection notices, the Getting Started step titles, which share the row titles' header case), so those are checked by reading, not by the test. `maskProtected` tries the longest protected phrase first, so `In-Chat Agents` is masked whole before `Agents` is.
- **A toast message or dialog body of one sentence ends without a period (HIG Periods):** 313 SillyBunny-written messages and bodies lost it, including strings that end in a template hole (`Deleted ${n} chat(s)`); text of two sentences or paragraphs, a message that ends in a file name (`Failed to save config.yaml.`) and upstream-verbatim text keep it. Tests that assert a message pass the new text, and `zh-cn.json` and `ru-ru.json` hold the period-free keys beside the old ones, with the old translated values, so a translation still ends in a period until a translator changes it.

### Prompt caching and cache markers

- **Files:** `src/prompt-caching.js`, `src/prompt-converters.js`, `src/endpoints/backends/chat-completions.js`, `src/endpoints/server-admin.js`, `public/scripts/local-url-utils.js`, `public/scripts/openai.js`, `public/scripts/sillybunny-server-request-settings.js`, `public/scripts/sillybunny-backend-pages.js`, `public/scripts/sillybunny-tabs.js`, `public/scripts/sillybunny-settings-presentation.js`.
- **Breaks:**
  - Issue `#1009` reported that Gemini 4 models on OpenRouter got no cache discount. Upstream's `cachingSystemPromptForOpenRouter()` marked the first `system` message it found, also a mid-chat one, and sent no `session_id`.
  - Upstream's depth functions wrapped an empty string into an empty text block, threw on an empty content array, and marked a trailing assistant prefill when a Post-History Instructions `system` message followed it.
  - `server-admin.js` read `YAMLMap` nodes as plain objects, so `/api/server-admin/config/chat-completions/get` always returned the defaults and a following save wrote them over the saved flags.
  - `claude.enableAdaptiveThinking` was a module constant of `chat-completions.js`, so a saved value applied only after a restart.
  - Found in review of PR `#1030`, before merge: a rule that placed markers only on main turns (`type !== 'quiet'`, `cacheScope` `main`) removed every marker from Conversation mode replies (`cacheScope: 'conversation-mode'`, sent as `quiet`) and from summaries and in-chat agents, which upstream marks; NanoGPT got `explicitCacheControl: true` with the system flag alone, which turns off NanoGPT's own breakpoints; mid-chat `system` items of the Responses API carried `input_image` parts, which upstream never sent; a catalog fetch that threw synchronously left the in-flight slot set, so the catalog was never fetched again.
  - The Server Config card in Presets (provider sub-headings, two explainers, Reload / Save / Save & Restart) and its later form (a confirmation Alert on every change) were replaced, because a save applies at once and needs no restart.
- **Guards:**
  - `src/prompt-caching.js` owns marker placement, the OpenRouter catalog lookup and the routing keys. `src/prompt-converters.js` keeps upstream's three exported functions and delegates to it.
  - Markers go on every request, as upstream; `cacheScope: 'none'` is the only opt-out (`isCachingDisabled`). `cacheScope` otherwise serves the local single-slot caches (`getLocalPromptCacheValue`), where a helper call would evict the main chat; a provider cache needs a breakpoint to read an entry.
  - `applySystemPromptCacheForOpenRouter()` marks the last non-empty message of the leading `system`/`developer` run and nothing when there is no such run. `applyDepthCacheBreakpoints()` skips `system`/`developer` messages before it looks for the trailing assistant prefill, and moves a marker to the next older message when the target cannot hold one (empty array, empty or whitespace text, a block type outside `CACHEABLE_CLAUDE_BLOCK_TYPES`).
  - At most four markers are added across `tools`, `system` and `messages`, and no `5m` marker is placed before a `1h` marker (Anthropic: "a 1-hour cache entry must appear before any 5-minute cache entries"). A seeded property test holds both. Markers that a request already carries are counted and never removed.
  - OpenRouter: Claude is matched by id (no catalog fetch). Gemini gets one marker, on the leading system prefix, without a `ttl`, when `gemini.enableSystemPromptCache` is on (OpenRouter's guide: "only the final one will be used for Gemini"). Any other model whose catalog entry has a cache-write price above zero, except an OpenAI model (OpenAI caches without markers), gets the Claude markers without a `ttl`, under the `claude.*` settings; the catalog is fetched only while one of them is on, cached for 15 minutes, shared by concurrent callers, and kept as a stale copy during an outage.
  - NanoGPT: with `claude.enableSystemPromptCache` and no depth, only the body helper `cache_control: { enabled, ttl }` is sent, as upstream; with a depth, the inline markers are placed and the helper carries `explicitCacheControl: true`.
  - Routing keys are an HMAC of the namespace, the user handle and `chat_id` (`deriveSessionCacheKey`), so the chat id never leaves the server. They are sent only when the request carries `chat_id`, the scope is not `none`, and, except for OpenRouter's `session_id`, no reverse proxy is set: `session_id` (OpenRouter), `prompt_cache_key` (OpenAI Chat Completions and Responses, Mistral, Azure OpenAI after its key whitelist), `x-grok-conv-id` (xAI). Legacy text completions get none. `shouldForwardChatId()` in `public/scripts/local-url-utils.js` decides which requests carry `chat_id`: every request of those sources and Fireworks unless its scope is `none`, and for Fireworks not a `quiet` one, as upstream. A quiet request needs the key of its chat: in a live check on OpenRouter (2026-11-25, Claude Haiku 5.5) a summary request without it went to another provider than its chat and paid a full cache write, and with it read 22,591 of 22,617 prompt tokens from the cache.
  - `convertMessagesToResponsesFormat()` lifts only the leading `system`/`developer` run into `instructions`; a later one stays in `input` with its role and its text parts only.
  - `server-admin.js` reads the seven mirrored keys with `document.getIn([...])`, keeps omitted keys on a partial save, copies the saved keys into the cached config object after the write (`applyChatCompletionConfigToCache`), and reports `envManaged`, the keys an environment variable sets. Environment variables keep precedence through `getConfigValue`. The cached object is never replaced, because other keys are read at start-up only.
  - `public/scripts/sillybunny-server-request-settings.js` builds the Prompt Caching group (`#sb-openai-prompt-caching`: Enable Prompt Caching, Caching at Depth, Extended Cache TTL) after the last request set, and the Adaptive Thinking, API Version and Thought Signatures rows in Request Options for the sources whose server code reads the key. A change saves at once as a partial body through a queue (one save at a time). The switch reads on while either system flag is true or the depth is 0 or more, and turning it off clears both flags and writes a depth of -1, because the server honours each key on its own. Turning it on writes `claude.enableSystemPromptCache` and the depth it had before it was turned off in this page session, or depth 0 otherwise, so the chat history is cached too. It does not turn on `gemini.enableSystemPromptCache`: in the same live check, Gemini 3.7 Flash on OpenRouter with the marker cached only the system prompt (5,239 tokens on each of six turns), and without it Gemini's own caching reached 20,410 of 25,792 prompt tokens by turn six; Gemini 4 Hydrogen ignored the marker. Nothing is read for a user who is not an admin, the rows leave the page when the read is refused, and a row whose key is in `envManaged` is insensitive. No key enters `settingsToUpdate`, so presets are not involved.
  - The first read waits until the Presets panel is shown, because a settings search builds the page and must start no `/api/server-admin/` request (`tests/startup-on-demand.e2e.js`). Placement is idempotent, because the drawer observer runs `updatePromptingPanel` again on every child mutation. `bindDependentSets` leaves a row that carries `data-sb-sensitivity-owned` to its owner.
- **Tests:** `tests/prompt-caching.test.js`, `tests/prompt-converters.test.js`, `tests/upstream-provider-parity.test.js`, `tests/unit/sillybunny-cache-routing.test.js`, `tests/unit/sillybunny-server-request-settings.test.js`, `tests/unit/sillybunny-dependent-sets.test.js`, `tests/prompt-caching-settings.e2e.js`, `tests/sillybunny-backend-settings.e2e.js`, `tests/sillybunny-settings-consistency.e2e.js`, `tests/startup-on-demand.e2e.js`, `tests/audit/settings-inventory.e2e.js`.

### Image generation reference state

- **Breaks:** Quick Image Gen saves a character's NovelAI Precise References in a configuration as the global ones, or makes them the global ones, when a configuration is saved or loaded while a character override is active (#872 review).
- **Cause:** during an override the live settings hold the character's values, and the global values are in `charSettingsBaseState` (in memory) and `_charSettingsBaseState` (saved with the settings). `getGlobalConfigurationSettings()` puts the global value back for each character-scoped key that it names, so a key that it misses goes into a saved configuration with the character's value, and `loadConfiguration()` then stores that value as the global base. `rememberCharSettingsBaseState()` writes both copies when a chat or a character loads outside an override, when an override is removed, and when a configuration is loaded or settings are imported during an override. An edit does not write them, so outside an override the saved copy can be older than the live value.
- **Files:** `public/scripts/extensions/quick-image-gen/index.js` (`cloneCharScopedState()`, `applyCharScopedStateToGenerationSettings()`, `clearCharacterReferenceSettings()`, `getScopedCharacterGenerationSettings()`, `applyCharScopedState()`, `getGlobalConfigurationSettings()`, `performLoadCharSettings()`), `public/scripts/extensions/quick-image-gen/lib/client-orchestration.js`.
- **Guards:** `getGlobalConfigurationSettings()` takes `naiPreciseReference` from `charSettingsBaseState` during an override and from the live settings otherwise. `cloneCharScopedState()`, `applyCharScopedStateToGenerationSettings()`, `clearCharacterReferenceSettings()`, `applyCharScopedState()` and `getGlobalConfigurationSettings()` name each character-scoped reference key, so a new one needs a line in each of them.
- **Breaks:** every NovelAI request from Image Generation threw when the saved `novel_precise_reference` was `null`, as a hand-edited settings file can make it. **Guard:** `generateNovelImage()` in `public/scripts/extensions/stable-diffusion/index.js` sends references only when the state has `enabled === true` and a `references` array.
- **Breaks:** the shared Precise Reference editor (`public/scripts/novelai-precise-reference-ui.js`) dropped the images that it prepared, with no message, when the reference list changed during preparation. It keeps them only when its container is in the page and `getState().references` is still the same array, because otherwise they belong to an older state. **Guards:** the toggle and the row controls are disabled during preparation, as Add and Remove were, and prepared PNGs are stored as user files (`/user/files/sb-nai-ref-*.png`) rather than inline base64 in `settings.json`. In Quick Image Gen a chat switch, a configuration load and a settings import can still replace the list, and the NovelAI section leaves the page when another provider is selected. The editor then drops the images, best-effort deletes their newly uploaded files, and its status line shows "The images were not added because the references changed while they were prepared. Add them again." **Limit:** a container that left the page shows the message when it returns. When the new state has Precise Reference off, the editor shows only the switch row and its subtitle, and turning the switch on clears the message, so the user never sees it.
- **Breaks:** mounting shared `.ds-*` rows inside `#qig-settings` let QIG's legacy blanket element selectors (`#qig-settings label`, `#qig-settings small`, `#qig-settings input:not([type="checkbox"])`, `#qig-settings .checkbox_label input[type="checkbox"]`) break switches, segmented toggles and coarse-pointer slider touch targets, and rebuilding the whole editor with `replaceChildren()` dropped keyboard focus to `<body>`. **Guards:** the editor builds from `sillybunny-editor/` factories (`createPrefGroup`, `createSwitchRow`, `createExpanderRow`, `createToggleRow`, `.ds-row-slider`, `createButtonRow`), reconciles rows in place with deterministic focus transfers on remove/add, commits slider values on `change` rather than every `input`, loads its trimmed stylesheet lazily via `loadStylesheetAsync` with a `?v=` key, and `quick-image-gen/style.css` scopes `.nai-precise-reference *` out of QIG's legacy element rules.
- **Breaks:** the shared Precise Reference editor did not follow DESIGN.md (#872 review). Turning the switch off removed the saved reference rows, the group had no heading, each opened reference held a red Remove button row, and the error text stayed grey because `#sb-settings-page .sb-settings-mounted-shell .ds-row .ds-row-subtitle` outranks `.nai-precise-reference__status.is-error .ds-row-subtitle`. **Guards:** `refresh()` keeps the reference rows, Add and the file input mounted and makes them insensitive while the switch is off (`insensitive = busy || !current.enabled`). The group's heading is the switch's title, an `h4`; upstream `#user-settings-block h4` and `#left-nav-panel h4` (`public/style.css`) make an `h4` an uppercase, tracked kicker, so `#sb-settings-page .sb-settings-mounted-shell .ds-pref-group-header > .ds-pref-group-heading` resets `text-transform` and `letter-spacing`. Remove is a flat icon button in each reference row's header (`createExpanderRow` `suffix`, `.nai-precise-reference__remove`), named "Remove Reference N". The status row takes the shared forms: `.ds-row.sb-server-note` with `data-tone="danger"` for an error, and `.ds-row.sb-inline-hint` (`public/css/sillybunny-tabs.css`) for an empty list. It is hidden while the switch is off and references exist, and `.nai-precise-reference__status[hidden]` sets `display: none` because `.ds-row` sets `display: flex`. The lazy stylesheet loads while the switch is on or references exist, so the startup request count does not change. **Tests:** `tests/novelai-precise-reference-editor.test.js` ("keeps saved references in view and insensitive while the switch is off", "names each Remove button and marks an error as a danger status line"). **Fix:** `0c53e5f25`.
- **Tests:** `tests/sillybunny-extension-media-integration.test.js` (configurations during an override, Image Generation with a missing or malformed state and user-file resolution, the `genNovelAI()` request, refusals and shared-file cleanup guard), `tests/novelai-image-generation.test.js` (route refusals, including data that is not a PNG, malformed base64 and a total above 25 MiB; its harness takes the server's 500 MB body limit so that such a request reaches the route), `tests/quick-image-gen-precise-reference.test.js`, `tests/data-maid-precise-reference.test.js`, `tests/novelai-precise-reference-editor.test.js` (jsdom unit suite for shared rows, focus survival, `change`-only commits, user-file upload/cleanup and dropped-image alert), `tests/novelai-precise-reference-editor.e2e.js` (both Image Generation and Quick Image Gen at 1440×900 and 390×844 coarse pointer).
- **Fixes:** `e85b32471` (configurations during an override), `ad8702967` (malformed saved state), `14d25a68d` (editor controls during preparation), `573bd6343` (message when the editor drops prepared images).

## Reverts and release problems

- Performance work caused the largest rollback: `0ecfa44e3` reverted 14 optimisation commits. iOS stayed at "Initializing", and the desktop Characters panel did not open.
- A revert can cause its own regression: `f027cd460` (#595) restored mobile CSS that a June baseline revert removed.
- Release promotion failed once. #866 promoted `staging` to `release` too early for v1.8.0, `5d49b0ad1` (#891) reverted it, and #892 added a workflow guard.
- Hotfixes followed v1.2.7, v1.2.8 and v1.8.1. A cluster of fixes from 12 to 16 April led to the v1.3.6 stability release.
- **Upstream minimum widths beat smaller fixed widths:** `body:not(.big-avatars) .avatar_collage { min-width: var(--avatar-base-width) }` (`public/css/toggle-dependent.css:213`, 50px) wins over a smaller `width`, so in Home's Recent Chats a group chat's collage was 50px beside 44px avatars and its text started 6px further in. The old Home used 60px avatars, so the minimum never showed. `.welcomeRecent .recentChat .avatar` sets `min-width: 0` (`public/css/sillybunny-home.css`). Test: `tests/home-layout.e2e.js` (group row matches an individual row).
